TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT框架，通过生成器与基于约束的验证器迭代配合，从缺乏明确时间锚点的临床叙述中重建结构化的症状时间线。在包含5,347条疫苗不良事件叙述的MedTempo基准上，CRAFT在四种LLM骨干模型上均一致提升了时间排序准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床叙述中的症状时间进展理解对疾病监测、安全监测和因果关系评估至关重要，但临床叙述很少提供明确的时间锚点。现有方法主要关注跨多次就诊和含时间戳记录中的成对关系分类，未解决从单一且锚点稀疏的报告中重建结构化症状轨迹的问题。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，由一个生成器与一个基于约束的验证器配对，通过针对性反馈迭代生成并精炼分阶段的症状时间线。同时构建了MedTempo新基准，包含5,347条疫苗不良事件叙述（覆盖三种COVID-19疫苗类型），其中3,166条报告具有专家验证的时间阶段标注。

Result: 在四种LLM骨干模型上的实验表明，CRAFT consistently（一致地）提升了时间排序准确性；消融分析进一步分离了生成器与验证器组件在不同模型能力水平下的各自贡献。

Conclusion: CRAFT框架有效解决了锚点稀疏临床叙述中症状时间线重建的问题，验证器-生成器协同迭代机制在不同LLM骨干上均展现出鲁棒的性能提升，为临床应用中的时间推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Understanding the temporal progression of symptoms in clinical narratives is critical for disease monitoring, safety surveillance, and causality assessment. Clinical narratives, however, rarely provide explicit temporal anchors. Current approaches to temporal information reasoning focus predominantly on pairwise relation classification across multi-visit and timestamp-rich records, leaving the reconstruction of structured symptom trajectories from individual anchor-sparse reports largely unaddressed. We propose CRAFT, an LLM framework that pairs a generator with a constraint-based verifier to iteratively produce and refine stage-wise symptom timelines through targeted feedback. We conduct evaluation on MedTempo, a new benchmark of 5,347 vaccine adverse-event narratives spanning three COVID-19 vaccine types, with expert-validated temporal stage annotations for 3,166 reports. Experiments across four LLM backbones demonstrate that CRAFT consistently improves temporal ordering accuracy, with ablation analysis isolating the contribution of generator and verifier components across model capability levels.

</details>


### [38] [FastThaiG2P: Lightning-fast Thai Grapheme-to-phoneme Conversion for Voice Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12814)
*Charin Polpanumas*

Main category: cs.CL

The approach achieves an average latency of 0.15 ms per utterance on a benchmark of 27,242 synthetically generated utterances, of which 30\% is spent on tokenization, 12\% on normalization, and 58\% on out-of-vocabulary fallbacks (0.5\% OOV rate). To demonstrate its effectiveness, we used FastThaiG2P to phonemize Som-TTS, an open dataset containing 20 hours of grapheme-and-audio pairs, then trained an 82M-parameter StyleTTS 2 model based on a Kokoro-TTS recipe. The resulting model vocalizes intelligible Thai speech suitable for prototyping and development at 0.25 real-time factor (4x real-time) with ONNX inference on CPU.

Abstract: Large language models often fail when answer options require combining atomic judgments under explicit logical operators, even when they judge the individual atoms correctly. An operator-constrained integer linear program then composes the calibrated scores into a single prediction. We evaluate on LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA and introduce LOGICAL-SATA, a reading-comprehension benchmark derived from SATA-Bench. Our framework improves Macro-F1 from 48.3 to 77.0 on the human-validated LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA split and from 47.0 to 75.6 on LOGICAL-SATA, with the largest gains on NEITHER/NOR.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了量化投资研究层面的递归自我改进（recursive self-improvement），提出了AQuA系统，包含两个独立的语言模型驱动研究系统：一个用于符号因子发现，一个用于可训练模型开发。两个系统各自独立闭环研究，用已验证的证据引导后续提案。因子系统在加密货币宇宙上达到约0.190的组合信息系数；模型系统在美国股票上达到+0.0843的每只股票信息系数，并转化为阈值多空策略，样本外夏普比率最高达+2.50，2021至2025年间每年均为正收益。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 在受限意义上，两个系统都在研究过程层面实现了递归自我改进，证明了语言模型驱动的自主系统能够在量化投资研究中通过保留已验证证据并进行迭代优化来持续提升研究质量。

Abstract: We study recursive self-improvement at the level of quantitative-investment research: whether an autonomous system can use evidence from earlier experiments to improve the hypotheses and candidates proposed in later iterations. We present AQuA, which comprises two separate language-model-driven research systems: one for symbolic factor discovery and one for trainable model development. The two systems do not share agents, memories, candidate spaces, or research state. Instead, each independently closes its own research loop by retaining validated evidence and using it to guide subsequent proposals. In this bounded sense, both systems implement recursive self-improvement at the level of the research process. Each system also uses its own sealed sandbox, which fixes the data splits, feature and label definitions, and evaluator while allowing the model to act only through constrained factor expressions or configuration diffs. The factor system, a manager-mediated multi-agent pipeline, discovers and combines factors into a signal that reaches a combined information coefficient of about $0.190$ on a crypto universe. The model system, a config-driven loop over a hybrid time-series architecture, reaches a per-stock information coefficient of $+0.0843$ on US equities and converts it into a threshold long/short strategy with a held-out Sharpe of up to $+2.50$ at a two-leg cost. The strategy is positive in every year from 2021 to 2025.

</details>


### [41] [Falsehood and Impossibility Are Different Directions in an AI's Representation of Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12852)
*Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了对多模态开源模型Gemma 3 4B IT的探索性激活研究，使用85个提示词（涵盖17个哲学类别和15个主题，每个主题以真命题、偶然假命题、不可能命题、语义异常和必然假命题五种形式呈现）。研究发现模型的输出层将偶然假命题与矛盾混淆，但激活空间显示出不同模式：线性真值探针能区分不可能命题与真命题（AUC 0.93），但无法区分不可能命题与假命题（AUC 0.20）；不可能性探针在保留主题族上以AUC 1.00区分必然假命题与偶然假命题。真值与不可能性方向接近正交，而不可能性方向部分重叠语义异常方向但可区分。稀疏自编码器特征重复了此几何结构。结论是：在该模型的激活空间中，必然假命题并非偶然假命题的极端情况，而是更接近语义异常类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言可以描述虚假的事态和根本不可能发生的事态，但目前尚不清楚AI模型是否在内部区分这些不同类型的失败。作者希望探究AI模型的内部表征是否区分"偶然假命题"（contingent falsehood）与"必然假命题"（necessary falsehood/不可能命题），从而为古老的哲学区分提供经验性注脚。

Method: 使用85个提示词，涵盖17个哲学家族和15个主题的模态集（每个主题以真命题、偶然假命题、不可能命题、语义异常、必然假命题五种形式表达）。采用线性真值探针、不可能性探针（在保留的主题族上评估）、计算真值与不可能性方向的正交性、比较不可能性与语义异常方向的重叠，并使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征在层15验证几何结构。

Result: 模型在回答中混淆偶然假命题与矛盾（15个假命题中12个被标记为"矛盾"），但激活空间呈现不同模式：真值探针区分不可能与真命题达到AUC 0.93，却无法区分不可能与假命题（AUC 0.20）；不可能性探针在层15以AUC 1.00区分必然与偶然假命题，平衡准确率0.97（Bonferroni校正P=0.018）。真值与不可能性方向接近正交；不可能性方向部分重叠语义异常方向但可区分。SAE特征重复此几何：不可能性选择性特征在异常句子上也激活，但很少在偶然假命题上激活。

Conclusion: 在该模型的激活空间中，必然假命题不是偶然假命题的极端情形，而是更接近语义异常这一实验性定义的类别。然而，这种表征上的接近并不意味着不可能语句本质上无意义。这些来自单个小型模型的相关性观察，为古老的哲学区分提供了一个经验性脚注。

Abstract: Language can describe states of affairs that are false and states of affairs that could not be the case at all. Whether an AI model internally distinguishes these failures remains unclear. I report an exploratory activation study of the multimodal open-weight model Gemma 3 4B IT using 85 prompts from 17 philosophical families and a topic-matched modality set of 15 topics, each expressed as a truth, contingent falsehood, improbable claim, semantic anomaly, and necessary falsehood. In its answers, the model conflates contingent falsehood with contradiction, labeling 12 of 15 false statements "contradiction." Its activations show a different pattern. A linear truth probe separates impossible from true statements (AUC 0.93) but not impossible from false statements (AUC 0.20). An impossibility probe evaluated on held-out topic families separates necessary from contingent falsehood at AUC 1.00, peaking at layer 15 with balanced accuracy 0.97 (Bonferroni-adjusted P=0.018). The truth and impossibility directions are close to orthogonal, whereas the impossibility direction partially overlaps a semantic anomaly direction while remaining distinguishable from it. Sparse autoencoder features at the same layer repeat this geometry. Features selective for impossibility also fire on anomalous sentences but rarely on contingent falsehoods. In this model's activation space, necessary falsehoods are not extreme cases of contingent falsehood but lie closer to the experimentally defined category of semantic anomaly. This representational proximity does not imply that impossible statements are intrinsically meaningless. These correlational observations from one small model offer an empirical footnote to an old philosophical distinction.

</details>


### [42] [The Embedder's Dilemma: LLMs Are Better, but at What Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12875)
*Adnan El Assadi,Niklas Muennighoff,Jinhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控、成本感知的大规模实验，系统比较了10个LLM（118M-14B参数）与26个嵌入模型在37个任务上的表现。两者总体表现几乎持平（77.6 vs 77.2），但优势领域不同：LLM在推理密集型检索任务上领先，嵌入模型在分类任务上领先，在聚类、STS和配对分类上两者持平。然而LLM成本极高，最高比同级嵌入模型贵1431倍，开放LLM在GPU上处理速度慢2.5-736倍，推理token占LLM推理成本的28-81%。帕累托前沿包含领先的嵌入模型和Gemini 3.1 Pro一个LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力的提升，一个重要问题浮现：是否应该用LLM替代传统的文本嵌入管道？此前缺乏大规模、成本感知的受控比较。

Method: 对10个LLM（涵盖6个家族）、26个嵌入模型（118M-14B参数）在37个任务（分类、STS、聚类、配对分类、检索）上进行受控的成本感知对比评估，并进行了推理预算消融实验。

Result: 两种范式总体表现几乎持平（0.4分差距）。LLM在检索上领先、嵌入模型在分类上领先、两者在其余任务上持平。但LLM成本是嵌入模型的数千倍，速度慢数百倍，推理token占总成本28-81%。帕累托前沿主要为嵌入模型，仅Gemini 3.1 Pro一个LLM入选。降低推理预算对多数模型无损甚至提升检索质量。

Conclusion: 建议采用分工策略：相似度、分类和聚类任务使用嵌入模型，推理密集型检索任务保留给LLM。

Abstract: Should you replace your text-embedding pipeline with a large language model? We answer this with a controlled, cost-aware comparison of ten LLMs across six families and 26 embedding models (118M to 14B parameters) on 37 tasks spanning classification, semantic textual similarity (STS), clustering, pair classification, and retrieval. In aggregate the two paradigms are effectively tied: the best LLM (Gemini 3.1 Pro, 77.6) and the best embedding model (77.2) differ by 0.4 points. Their strengths differ by task: LLMs lead on reasoning-heavy retrieval, embedding models lead on classification, and the two match on clustering, STS, and pair classification. Reaching that parity is expensive. An LLM costs up to 1,431x more than an embedding model of comparable quality (USD 154 vs. USD 0.11 per benchmark pass), and the open LLMs tested process tokens 2.5 to 736x more slowly on the same GPU. Reasoning tokens account for 28 to 81% of LLM inference cost; lower reasoning budgets preserve or improve retrieval quality for most models in our ablation. The Pareto frontier contains the leading embedding models and one LLM, Gemini 3.1 Pro. These results support a division of labour: use embedding models for similarity, classification, and clustering, and reserve LLMs for reasoning-intensive retrieval. Our code, datasets, and results are publicly available at https://github.com/embeddings-benchmark/embedders-dilemma.

</details>

*Ruizhe Li,Licheng Zhang,Benfeng Xu,Mingxuan Du,Zheren Fu,Weidong Chen*

TL;DR: ReFind是一个不构建任何语义结构的代理控制搜索接口，通过保留原始对话存档、词法索引和迭代关键词搜索，在记忆检索任务上超越了图/树结构的记忆系统（58.2 vs HippoRAG 2的53.2），表明结构化的收益大多可通过可控搜索从未修改的记录中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent记忆系统倾向于将原始对话历史转换为摘要、嵌入、树或知识图谱等结构以提升检索质量，但作者质疑这些收益究竟来自结构本身，还是来自对原始历史的有效检索。因此希望揭示结构化的真实价值。

Conclusion: 对精确、基于证据的对话存档问题，归功于复杂记忆结构的大部分收益可以通过给代理一个可控搜索接口（作用于未修改的记录）来恢复，且完全不需要基于LLM的索引构建。

Abstract: Agent-memory systems increasingly buy retrieval quality with structure, transforming raw conversation histories into summaries, embeddings, trees, or knowledge graphs before any question is asked. We ask how much of that benefit comes from the structure itself, rather than from competent retrieval over the raw history. We present ReFind, an agent-controlled search interface that builds no semantic structure at all: it leaves the conversation archive unmodified, indexes it lexically at turn granularity, and combines a generic iterative keyword-search loop with four chat-native controls grounded in empirical refinding work: session-aware rank fusion, local context expansion, temporal narrowing, and skipping already-inspected sessions. A separate reasoning stage answers from the collected evidence. Across a broad suite of conversational-memory tasks (single- and multi-hop QA, event ordering, and fact consolidation), roughly 2,800 questions on precise-retrieval and fact-tracking capabilities evaluated under the incremental multi-turn setting of MemoryAgentBench, ReFind attains the highest mean accuracy (58.2) of any system compared, above the strongest graph- and tree-based memory systems (HippoRAG 2, 53.2), all under a GPT-4o-mini backbone matched to every reused baseline. Controlled comparisons to single-shot BM25, a matched generic-agentic BM25 control, component removals, and agentic dense/hybrid variants separately support the roles of agent control, chat-native controls, and lexical retrieval. On LongMemEval-S/M, the same interface reaches 93.2 +/- 3.3 and 89.3 +/- 6.0 with GPT-5-mini. The results indicate that for precise, evidence-grounded questions over chat archives, much of the benefit credited to elaborate memory structures is recoverable by giving an agent controllable search over the unmodified record, with no LLM-based index construction at all.

</details>

*Jophin John,Michael Hoffmann,Jan Fillies,Michael A. Hedderich,Barbara Plank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BavGround基准，用于评估大语言模型在巴伐利亚地区文化接地与方言能力上的表现，涵盖英语、德语和巴伐利亚语三种语言、206道多选题目，并发现不同评估协议会显著影响模型评分与排名。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文化评估多聚焦于高资源标准语言，忽视了地区文化和方言社区。巴伐利亚方言作为低资源语言及其独特的地区文化知识缺乏系统性评估基准，需要填补这一空白。

Result: 强多语言模型总体表现最佳，但在巴伐利亚语题目和来源接地问题上性能下降。评估协议的选择会显著影响绝对分数与排名，尤其对地区适配模型影响更大。对GENBA-10B检查点的探索分析显示，持续预训练不均地提升各领域的答案内容似然，但方言能力仍相对薄弱。

Conclusion: BavGround为LLM的地区文化表征提供了本地化、协议感知的评估支持，揭示了模型在方言和地域文化知识上的持续挑战，并强调评估协议标准化的重要性。

Abstract: Cultural evaluation of large language models (LLMs) often focuses on high-resource standard languages, leaving regional culture and dialect communities underrepresented. We introduce BavGround, a benchmark for evaluating Bavarian regional cultural grounding and dialect competence across English, German and Bavarian. BavGround contains 206 multiple-choice source questions across eight cultural domains per language, yielding 618 multi-parallel instances, with items covering both broadly accessible cultural knowledge and source-grounded regional knowledge from journalism, historical sources, and specialist literature. We evaluate fifteen 7B-10B open-weight instruction-tuned models and one closed-model reference. Strong multilingual models perform best overall, but performance drops on Bavarian items and source-grounded questions, indicating persistent difficulty with dialectal and localized cultural knowledge. We further show that conclusions depend strongly on evaluation protocol: raw answer-letter scoring, shuffled-letter scoring, option-text likelihood, generated-answer parsing, and semantic matching can produce different absolute scores and rankings, especially for regionally adapted models. Finally, an exploratory analysis of GENBA-10B checkpoints suggests that continued pretraining improves answer-content likelihood unevenly across domains, while dialect competence remains comparatively weak. BavGround supports localized, protocol-aware evaluation of cultural representation in LLMs.

</details>

*Victoria Basmov,Yoav Goldberg,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从GitHub收集了57.5K个独特的交易型提示（transactional prompts）样本，提出了一种结构化本体论来描述提示的属性和形式/语义组件，将非结构化的原始文本转化为结构化的语言对象，并进行了多样性和错误分析，最后发布了数据集及浏览界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代生成式大语言模型（LLM）的行为直接由提示（prompts）塑造，但提示作为语言对象本身却很少被系统地研究。作者认为提示值得作为独立的语言学课题进行深入调查，因此需要大规模、结构化的语料库和本体来完成这一目标。

Method: 从GitHub收集57.5K个独特的交易型提示样本（可复现的自然语言指令，集成于软件中）；引入结构化本体论，捕捉提示的属性及其形式与语义组件；基于该本体将原始非结构化文本转化为结构化语言对象；进行全面错误分析以验证本体注释的可靠性。

Result: 分析揭示了提示在语言、领域、任务和模态上的显著多样性，呈现典型的Zipf分布——少数提示占主导，更多样化的提示出现在长尾中。错误分析提供了各字段注释质量的详细评估。数据集和浏览界面已发布。

Conclusion: 提示是值得独立研究的语言学对象，所提出的本体论和大规模结构化数据集为提示的实证、定量研究奠定了基础，有助于更深入地理解LLM提示的使用模式和语言特征。

Abstract: The behavior of contemporary generative Large Language Models (LLMs) is directly shaped by prompts, unstructured texts that describe the desired output and model behavior. In this paper we argue that prompts are linguistic objects that merit investigation in their own right. To this end, we collect 57.5K unique samples of prompts from GitHub. Specifically, we focus on transactional prompts: reproducible natural language instructions that are integrated into software. To enable the empirical, quantitative study of prompts, we introduce a structured ontology, capturing the properties of prompts as well as their formal and semantic components. Based on this ontology, we transform prompts from unstructured raw texts into richly structured linguistic objects. Analysis of these structured data reveals significant diversity of usage patterns across languages, domains, tasks, and modalities, in a typical Zipf-like distribution where some clearly prevail and others, more diverse, appear in the long tail. To validate the reliability of the ontology-based annotation of the prompts, we perform a comprehensive error analysis across all fields, providing a detailed assessment of annotation quality. We release the dataset together with a browsing and exploration interface (https://github.com/OnlpLab/transactionalPromptsCollection ).

</details>


### [46] [Decoupled Contrastive Decoding via Expert-Aligned Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12913)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuanfu Wang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦对比解码（DCD），将对比解码中的草稿器与验证器解耦：使用专家对齐的轻量级提议器进行草稿生成，仅在验证阶段应用业余模型。实验表明，对比感知的草稿生成并不比专家对齐草稿生成更优，而DCD在保持原始CD输出分布的同时，实现了1.65-1.95倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比解码（CD）虽能提升生成质量，但业余模型参与使得解码成本高昂。使用推测解码加速CD时面临一个关键问题：对比信号应作用于草稿器还是仅保留在验证阶段？作者希望解答这一提议对齐问题，以在保持CD质量的前提下实现高效解码。

Method: 作者在轻量级特征级草稿器机制下设计了两个受控诊断实验：匹配Cross-alpha训练和近似双草稿器分解。基于诊断结果，提出解耦对比解码（DCD）：使用专家对齐的轻量级提议器进行草稿生成，仅在标准的CD验证阶段应用业余模型，从而保持vanilla-CD输出分布不变。

Result: 诊断结果表明对比感知草稿生成并不一致性地优于专家对齐草稿生成，因为对比修正通常弱于草稿器误差，且重建过程可能放大该误差。在主要的8B设置下，基于EAGLE3的DCD在vanilla CD基础上实现了平均1.65-1.95倍的贪心加速，并将MMLU提议路径延迟相对业余模型耦合的提议路径降低约5-12倍。

Conclusion: DCD通过解耦草稿与验证过程，在保持CD输出分布的同时显著提升了解码效率，验证了在轻量级草稿器机制下，对比信号应保留在验证阶段而非作用于草稿器设计的核心主张。

Abstract: Contrastive Decoding (CD) improves generation quality, but its amateur-model pass makes decoding expensive. Accelerating CD with speculative decoding raises a proposal-alignment question: should the contrastive signal shape the drafter, or should it remain only in verification? We study this question in the lightweight feature-level drafter regime. Two controlled diagnostics, matched Cross-alpha training and an Approximate Dual-Drafter decomposition, give the same diagnosis: contrastive-aware drafting does not consistently improve over expert-aligned drafting because the contrastive correction is usually weaker than drafter error, and reconstruction can amplify that error. We introduce Decoupled Contrastive Decoding (DCD), which drafts with an expert-aligned lightweight proposer and applies the amateur only in unchanged CD verification. Standard speculative verification preserves the vanilla-CD output distribution. Across the main 8B settings, EAGLE3-based DCD achieves average greedy speedups of 1.65 to 1.95x over vanilla CD and reduces MMLU proposal-path latency by about 5 to 12x relative to amateur-coupled proposal paths.

</details>


### [47] [Unifying Depth and Width Pruning for LLMs via Binary Knapsack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12953)
*Palaash Goel,Ayan Sengupta,Akshay Nambi,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SNIPER是一个两阶段结构化剪枝框架，先通过背包优化对粗粒度组件进行条件最优参数分配，再进行细粒度剪枝以满足严格的预算约束。与现有剪枝方法相比，SNIPER实现了近乎精确的压缩比达标（CRAFT得分0.98），在18个任务、5个领域、4种架构上平均排名1.25，优于六种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法严重依赖贪婪启发式算法，产生短视决策，且往往无法精确达到目标压缩预算。这些方法在参数分配上缺乏全局最优性，导致压缩效果不理想。

Method: SNIPER采用两阶段框架：第一阶段将粗粒度组件的剪枝建模为背包优化问题，在固定重要性估计下获得条件最优的参数分配方案；第二阶段进行细粒度剪枝以严格满足预算约束。同时引入压缩比遵循因子（CRAFT）来量化预算保真度。

Result: 现有剪枝器偏离目标压缩比高达33%，而SNIPER实现近乎精确的遵循，CRAFT得分达0.98。在4种架构、18个任务、5个领域上的评估中，SNIPER在平均性能保留和任务级稳定性上均优于六种最先进的剪枝方法，跨所有剪枝配置的平均排名为1.25。

Conclusion: SNIPER展示了跨架构的鲁棒泛化能力和优秀的可靠性，其背包优化驱动的两阶段剪枝框架有效解决了现有方法在预算精度和决策全局性方面的缺陷，为大语言模型结构化剪枝提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for compressing large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods rely heavily on greedy heuristics that produce myopic decisions, and often fail to precisely meet target compression budgets. We present SNIPER, a two-stage structured pruning framework that solves a knapsack optimization over coarse-granularity components to yield conditionally optimal parameter allocations with respect to fixed importance estimates, followed by a fine-grained pruning stage to meet strict budget constraints. We introduce the Compression Ratio Adherence Factor (CRAFT) to quantify budget fidelity, showing that while existing pruners deviate from target compression ratios by up to 33%, SNIPER achieves near-exact adherence with a CRAFT score of 0.98. Evaluations across four diverse architectures over a set of 18 tasks spanning five domains demonstrate SNIPER's consistent improvements in average performance retention and task-level stability over six state-of-the-art pruners. Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [48] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LycheeMemory V2 提出了一种高效的长期记忆框架，用语义分段级整合替代了逐轮整合，通过批量处理多个交互并编码为上下文无关的类型化记忆记录，显著降低构建成本，同时保持甚至提升记忆检索准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长期记忆系统通常依赖"急切整合"（eager consolidation），即在每次交互后调用LLM提取、总结或更新记忆，导致对话增长时记忆构建成本不断上升。粗粒度摘要虽然能降低成本，但可能丢失细粒度的上下文证据；而增大检索上下文或多跳推理又会将开销转移到查询阶段。作者希望找到记忆整合粒度与成本-精度权衡之间的更优平衡点。

Method: LycheeMemory V2 用语义分段级整合（semantic segment-level consolidation）替代逐轮整合。具体而言：(1) 将多个交互批量组合成语义片段，仅在片段结束时进行LLM编码，降低LLM调用频率；(2) 通过语义边界检测保持事件级和时间级证据的一致性，优于固定窗口批处理；(3) 将最终片段编码为上下文无关的类型化记忆记录；(4) 使用轻量级结构化索引支持查询计划的证据检索。

Result: 使用GPT-4.1-Mini进行评估，LycheeMemory在LoCoMo上达到89.22%的准确率，在LongMemEval-S上达到92.20%，实现了最先进性能。与A-Mem相比，在LoCoMo上减少86.0%的构建token，在LongMemEval-S上减少75.9%，且不增加查询阶段token消耗。

Conclusion: 长期智能体记忆的精度-成本权衡不仅取决于保留什么信息，还取决于信息整合的粒度（granularity）。段级语义整合在保持高精度的同时大幅降低记忆构建成本，为长期记忆系统设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [49] [HybridRAG-BN: A Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Fine-Tuned Verification for Bangla KBQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13004)
*Rathijit Aich,Nirjhar Das,Mahfuzulhoq Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出HybridRAG-BN，一个面向孟加拉语知识库问答（KBQA）的检索增强框架，结合BM25和BGE-M3的混合检索、GGUF版Gemma大模型生成答案、以及LoRA微调模型进行答案验证与改进，在比赛中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的KBQA系统面临检索研究不足、语言资源稀缺以及在外部知识中锚定生成答案的困难，亟需可靠的检索增强问答框架。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1) 使用BM25和BGE-M3的混合检索机制；2) 使用GGUF版本的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct生成答案；3) 通过LoRA微调的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct模型进行答案验证和细化。此外，还包含后处理阶段，通过回退答案替换和DuckDuckGo辅助检索来处理未解决的案例。

Result: 在公开和私有排行榜上分别取得0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，在竞赛中排名第一。

Conclusion: HybridRAG-BN有效证明了混合检索结合大模型生成与微调验证的框架能够显著提升低资源语言KBQA的性能，为孟加拉语问答系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge-base question answering (KBQA) systems rely on effective retrieval and reasoning mechanisms to generate accurate answers from external knowledge sources. However, developing reliable KBQA systems for low-resource languages such as Bangla remains challenging due to limited retrieval-focused research, scarce language resources, and difficulties in grounding generated responses in external knowledge. In this work, we propose HybridRAG-BN, a retrieval-augmented framework for Bangla KBQA that integrates hybrid retrieval using BM25 and BGE-M3, answer generation using the GGUF version of Gemma-4-31B-Instruct, and a LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma-4-31B-Instruct model for answer verification and refinement. To further improve robustness, the framework incorporates a post-processing stage that addresses unresolved cases through fallback answer replacement and DuckDuckGo-assisted retrieval. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving token-level F1 scores of 0.71654 and 0.72912 on the public and private leaderboards, respectively, securing first place in the competition.

</details>


### [50] [EviReform: Evidence-Guided Query Reformulation for Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13006)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviReform是一种图检索方法，它将检索请求的修订与图证据聚合分离。检索到的源段落会为未解决的信息需求生成残差查询，原始与残差检索信号分别归一化后组合，并在共享实体的命题间传播。在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue上，EviReform分别比最强基线高出最多5.59个Recall@5点和4.50个F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索需要恢复能够共同提供充分证据的段落。初始段落常隐含地解析问题中的实体或关系，导致缺失证据只有在检索开始后才易于描述。现有图检索的检索信号通常来源于原始问题，互补证据必须通过存储的关系才能到达，即使观测到的段落提供了更直接的语义线索。

Method: EviReform将修订检索请求与在图中的证据聚合分离。检索到的源段落为未解决的信息需求制定残差查询；原始与残差检索信号被分开归一化、组合，并在共享实体的命题之间传播。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue数据集上，EviReform在Recall@5指标上最高超过最强基线5.59点，在F1指标上最高超过4.50点。

Conclusion: 观测到的证据可以引导图检索走向原始问题未充分指定的支持链部分，表明利用已检索证据反馈来动态修订检索请求能有效提升多跳证据检索效果。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval must recover passages that provide sufficient evidence together. An initial passage often resolves an entity or relation implicit in the question, making the missing evidence easier to describe only after retrieval begins. Graph retrieval improves access to related evidence through stored corpus structure, but its retrieval signal is commonly derived from the original question. Complementary evidence must then be reached through stored relations even when an observed passage provides a more direct semantic cue. We introduce EviReform, which separates revising the retrieval request from aggregating evidence in the graph. Retrieved source passages formulate residual queries for the unresolved information need. The original and residual retrieval signals are normalized separately, combined, and propagated between propositions that share entities. On 2WikiMultiHopQA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, EviReform exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 5.59 Recall@5 points and 4.50 F1 points. These results show that observed evidence can guide graph retrieval toward the part of a supporting chain left underspecified by the original question. Code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/EviReform.

</details>


### [51] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGSieve 是一种自引用的检索增强生成（RAG）投毒检测框架，包含查询端（RSQ）和语料端（RSG）两种检测机制。RSQ 通过查询局部对比（比较 top-5 与排名 6-20 的检索结果）捕捉答案锚定集中与载体转换；RSG 通过语料局部对比（比较语义相似但词汇不同的邻居与局部基线）在查询前检测协同密度。在三个 QA 数据集和六种投毒构造下，联合部署将攻击成功率从 67.4% 降至 14.0%，无需投毒标签或可信语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 将外部语料作为推理证据，攻击者可注入文档使模型倾向选择攻击者指定的答案。现有检测方法依赖可信参考、特定攻击痕迹或对语料拓扑敏感的全局阈值，缺乏普适性和实用性。

Method: 提出 RAGSieve 自引用检测框架：(1) RSQ 在查询时执行查询局部对比，将 top-5 候选与同一检索中排名 6-20 的结果进行对比，检测答案锚定集中（answer-anchor concentration）和载体转换（carrier transitions）；(2) RSG 在语料摄入时机执行语料局部对比，将每个文档语义相似但词汇不同的邻居与局部基线比较，检测协同密度（coordinated density），无需投毒标签或可信语料。

Result: 在三个 QA 数据集和六种投毒构造下：RSQ 达到 95.2% AUROC，在 5% 干净文档移除条件下检测出 82.2% 投毒（对比 GMTP 的 81.1%/52.5%）；RSG 达到 93.3%/79.8%（对比 CleanBase 的 79.4%/37.6%）。联合部署将攻击成功率从 67.4% 降至 14.0%，同时保持未投毒检索上 41.3% 的 F1。

Conclusion: RAGSieve 通过构建自引用的局部参考，在不需要可信参考语料和投毒标签的情况下，在语料摄入和查询两个阶段提供实用、有效的 RAG 投毒防护，代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [52] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CASA，一种结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B的简单架构，在自动口语评估任务上达到最先进性能（RMSE 0.358），同时提供更好的语音表达与内容之间的可解释性分离，并使用约一半的推理参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态语音大语言模型在自动口语评估中的应用缺乏对声学与内容信息贡献的深入分析，且性能稳定性未被充分研究。此外，现有架构往往过于复杂且可解释性不足。

Method: 提出CASA架构，采用Whisper-medium处理声学信息与Qwen3.5-2B处理内容信息，实现语音表达与内容评估的可解释分离；使用三个手工设计的流利度特征；通过消融实验和重复运行分析各模态信息的独立与互补贡献；利用大语言模型推理实现免训练的内容验证。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上，CASA的RMSE达到0.358，优于此前最佳结果，同时推理参数量约为之前方法的一半。消融实验揭示了声学与内容信息的独立及互补贡献，并展示了性能变异性分析结果。

Conclusion: CASA证明了简单的通用架构可以在自动口语评估中同时实现高性能和可解释性，并具备跨语料库的适应性潜力，为后续研究提供了更清晰的多模态贡献分析框架。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [53] [LigBench: A Unified and Human-Aligned Benchmark for LLM-based Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13136)
*Chenrun Wang,Mingxuan Zhu,Tiancheng Huang,Wenjie Li,Yujie Zhang,Zichen Zhu,Zhiying Zou,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LigBench，一个用于自动化评估AI研究想法的基准测试，以及PAIR-IQ数据集用于训练成对想法判断模型，以解决现有研究想法生成评估缺乏客观标准和统一可靠性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在研究想法生成方面的应用日益增多，现有的评估实践碎片化且缺乏客观标准，通常依赖直接的LLM打分，无法在不同生成分布下提供统一可靠的评估。

Method: 提出LigBench自动化评估基准，支持细粒度且可靠的AI研究想法评估，可一致适用于不同生成分布；同时引入PAIR-IQ数据集，用于训练成对想法判断模型，作为辅助参考以支持更客观的比较评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，LigBench实现了稳定且可解释的评估，显著提升了与专家判断的一致性；基于PAIR-IQ训练的模型展现出更强的排序准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LigBench和PAIR-IQ为可扩展、客观的研究想法评估建立了原则性标准，为研究想法生成领域提供了统一可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), research idea generation has attracted increasing attention. Existing approaches enable LLMs to retrieve relevant literature and propose novel ideas for research areas. However, current evaluation practices for idea generation remain fragmented and lack objective standards, often relying on direct LLM scoring, which limits their ability to provide unified and reliable assessments across a coherent distribution of generated ideas. To address this challenge, we propose LigBench, an automated evaluation benchmark that enables fine-grained and reliable evaluation of AI research ideas, consistently applicable across different generation distributions. In addition, we introduce PAIR-IQ, a dataset tailored for training pairwise idea judgment models and serving as an auxiliary reference to support more objective comparative evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LigBench achieves stable and interpretable evaluations, significantly improving alignment with expert judgments. Furthermore, models trained on PAIR-IQ exhibit enhanced ranking accuracy and robustness, establishing a principled standard for scalable and objective research idea assessment.

</details>


### [54] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Syfer框架，一种合成器折叠框架，用于多语言多跳问答。它延迟翻译而非默认应用翻译，首先用格式约束分解器生成原语言的子问题图，进行分解质量检查，通过时按检索-回答策略用目标语言顺序回答子问题，失败时才激活英语翻译路径和双语子问题图对齐。实验表明在多种语言上达到竞争性准确率，并在性能与计算成本间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG方法存在两个局限：一是统一翻译对齐（一刀切式翻译）丢弃了目标语言特有的文化和语言原生信息，引入翻译噪声并增加系统成本；二是贪婪分解与聚合，无控制的分解产生冗余子问题，在逐步推理中累积错误，最终聚合进一步放大这些错误。

Method: Syfer框架包含：1) 格式约束分解器在原语言中生成子问题图；2) 分解质量检查机制；3) 检查通过时，在目标语言下按检索-回答策略顺序回答子问题；4) 检查失败时，激活英语翻译路径配合双语子问题图对齐。

Result: 在多种语言的实验中，Syfer达到了竞争性的准确率，同时在性能与计算成本之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: 延迟翻译而非默认应用翻译的策略是有效的，通过按需启动翻译路径可以在保持准确率的同时降低计算成本并保留语言原生信息。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [55] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ECBench基准，引入成人依恋理论（ECR-R量表）评估大语言模型在情感陪伴场景中的依恋焦虑与回避倾向，覆盖四个场景、11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法，对32个LLM进行测试并探索通过提示词调整依恋倾向的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估主要刻画一般人格特质，缺乏对亲密关系及情感敏感情境中模型行为的洞察，无法满足LLM日益广泛的情感陪伴应用需求。

Method: 将成人依恋理论引入LLM评估，使用ECR-R量表表征依恋焦虑与回避；构建涵盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交指导四个场景的ECBench基准（包含友谊与恋爱关系）；采用11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法，测试32个LLM并通过提示词探索依恋倾向的可塑造性。

Result: 评估了32个LLM的依恋倾向，选出代表性模型研究其在上下文多轮交互中的依恋表现，并验证了通过提示词调整依恋倾向的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解和选择用于情感陪伴的LLM提供了心理学理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [56] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEM是一个生成式嵌入模型，通过显式推理用户意图和相关性标准来增强检索，在单一模型中统一生成与嵌入功能，先对查询进行推理再嵌入编码，在推理密集和指令跟随检索任务上表现优于非推理变体，并可利用测试时计算扩展进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM擅长推理和指令遵循，使用户能够表达复杂多样的信息需求，但传统检索器主要依赖查询与文档之间的表面级匹配，导致用户表达需求的方式与检索器理解方式之间存在日益增长的差距。

Method: 提出GEM生成式嵌入模型，在单一模型中统一生成与嵌入：首先基于自身知识对查询进行推理（理解用户意图和相关性标准），然后附加嵌入token对丰富化后的上下文进行编码以用于检索。

Result: 在推理密集型和指令跟随检索任务上，GEM展示了推理增强检索的有效性，优于其非推理变体，并且匹配了使用显著更大模型的基线性能；此外其生成特性允许通过提示实现测试时计算扩展以进一步提升检索性能。

Conclusion: GEM证明了将推理能力整合到检索模型中能够弥合用户意图表达与检索器理解之间的鸿沟，推理增强检索是一种有效且可扩展的检索范式。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [57] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VLSR是一个端到端框架，通过"先定位后推理"策略，从分子图像中先定位化学上有意义的区域，再在紧凑的隐空间中进行性质推理，相比文本推理基线实现了9.6倍的高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM化学推理方法要么依赖SMILES/分子图像加局部基序描述，要么直接对分子图像推理，都无法让模型在推理前聚焦于化学上有意义的区域，缺乏定位与推理的联合学习能力。

Method: 提出Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR)，一个端到端框架，采用局部化学感知（localize-then-reason）策略：先学习在分子图像中定位化学意义区域，再在紧凑的隐空间工作区中推理这些区域的性质效应，最后生成最终答案。

Result: 在相同的推理设置下，VLSR相比可比较的文本推理基线实现了9.6倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: VLSR通过联合学习定位和推理，使模型能够在推理前聚焦于化学上有意义的区域，并以更高效率完成化学性质推理任务。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [58] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文量化了大型语言模型在自我指涉提示（引发主观体验报告）下的响应不稳定性，发现其不稳定性（0.343）显著高于不可解析的哲学问题（0.192）和可验证问题（0.105），表明这类主观体验报告在模型的输出分布中占据一个独特的、更不稳定的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已表明自我指涉提示能可靠地诱导LLM产生类似主观体验的第一人称报告，但从未有工作测量这些报告在重复独立试验中的一致性，也未与模型在其他开放式问题上的行为进行比较。作者旨在填补这一空白，为所谓"诱导主观体验"提供定量基线。

Method: 将响应不稳定性定义为一减去每个回答中提取的压缩核心声明的句子嵌入的平均成对余弦相似度。设置三组问题：自我指涉提示（引发主观体验报告）、与自我无关的不可解析哲学问题、以及有可验证正确答案的问题。每组4个问题，每问题30次独立响应（共360个响应），使用Gemini API，温度设为0.7。

Result: 自我指涉问题表现出最高的不稳定性（0.343 ± 0.047），不可解析哲学问题为中等且紧密聚集的不稳定性（0.192 ± 0.008），可验证问题为最低不稳定性（0.105 ± 0.058）。三组之间存在清晰的层级差异。

Conclusion: 自我指涉提示引发的主观体验报告在模型输出分布中占据一个独特且更不稳定的位置，其不稳定性显著高于普通的开放式哲学不确定性。这为诱导主观体验报告提供了定量基线，表明这类报告与常规的开放性哲学问题在输出稳定性上存在本质区别。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [59] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigBench，一个用于科学图表理解的诊断性VLM基准，同时评估感知、推理和不确定性下的行为可靠性。包含250个图、600+小时人工标注、34,000+个测试设置，并提出A-R-I框架评估模型是否承认证据不足、抵抗误导、谨慎推理。发现高感知和推理能力不等于行为可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准过度关注感知和推理准确率，忽视了不确定性下的行为可靠性（如视觉证据缺失或误导时模型如何表现），而这一维度对科学工作流中的实际部署至关重要。

Method: 构建SciFigBench基准：250个科学图表+人工标注，通过图像变换、推理问题、抵抗探针、标题偏差探针和选择性模糊目标扩展至34,000+评估设置。提出A-R-I（Admittance-Resistance-Inductance）框架，分别评估模型承认证据不足、抵抗误导语境、从部分信息谨慎推理的能力。

Result: GPT-5.2描述质量最高（MQM 91.6）、推理准确率78.4%，但在96%的不可读内容案例中产生幻觉；Gemini 3.1 Pro能力相当（MQM 90.2、推理81.0%），却在71%此类案例中承认不确定性，抵抗得分0.91最高。模型间行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 高感知和推理准确率并不能保证行为可靠性。对科学工作流部署而言，不确定性下的行为可靠性（承认不确定性、抵抗误导、谨慎推理）是与感知推理能力同等重要的关键维度，应纳入VLM评估体系。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [60] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出混合训练（MoT）框架，将Transformer预训练分解为可在独立对齐器支架中训练的连续层块，然后重组为完整模型。在1.3B参数Gemma风格模型上验证了可行性，达到与单体基线相同的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练能否被分解为更小的、可独立训练的任务，并在之后重新组合成连贯的大型模型，以研究分块训练作为可重用训练单元的潜力。

Method: MoT将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块，每个块在冻结的预训练对齐器支架内独立训练，训练完成后通过可选的短端到端适配阶段重组各块。

Result: 在1.3B参数Gemma风格模型和C4数据集上，MoT证明深度切片可重组为可用模型，质量对齐设置达到与单体基线相同的困惑度；但该设置处理的总token更多，有效计算优势取决于跨运行复用对齐器。

Conclusion: MoT并非作为单体预训练的通用替代方案，而是作为小规模框架，用于研究支架式子运行能否作为可重用的训练单元。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [61] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WIFA方法，通过自动构造"包装型有害示例+结构匹配的包装型良性反例"的意图组增强数据，配合两种互补微调路线（WIFA-Boost与A-GCRT），在提升转换后有害请求拒绝率的同时缓解过度拒绝问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调虽能提升有害拒绝率，但模型容易学习表面形式的捷径：包装后的有害提示可绕过安全机制，而包装后的良性提示又被过度拒绝。现有方法缺乏自动构造结构匹配的意图组数据的手段。

Method: 提出Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA)，自动构建包装有害示例与结构匹配的包装良性反例组成的意图组，无需外部教师或手动包装意图标签。WIFA作为公共数据层支撑两条互补微调路线：WIFA-Boost（两阶段高安全配方）和A-GCRT（对同意图包装的拒绝/遵从决策分数做正则化，并在边界两侧锚定有害与良性组）。

Result: 在Qwen设置下，WIFA-Boost达到最强的转换有害拒绝率；A-GCRT将OR-Bench过度拒绝率从基线的25.7%降至17.4%；复现的基线无法匹配这些工作点。Llama结果与消融实验支持意图组解释。

Conclusion: WIFA的意图组数据构造与两种训练路线共同验证了安全微调中的意图组解释，有效缓解表面形式捷径问题，但不主张普遍优于基线的过度拒绝表现。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协议级可辨识性审计方法，用于在零模型调用的情况下检查LLM基准测试是否能够区分具有不同目标属性的策略。在一个冻结的确定性策略类上，该审计发现基于基础观测的协议将七个策略塌缩为一个等价类，而完整支持可以正确区分；经验上，基础准确率与选择性响应保真度存在显著分歧（0.620 vs 0.324），且审计还合成了最小识别支持集（2格而非36格）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准分数即使在观测协议无法识别其所声称测量的行为属性时也可能表现得精确，导致评估有效性难以保证。现有方法缺乏在推理前对评估设计进行结构性检验的手段。

Method: 形式化了一个协议级可辨识性审计：在有限行为策略类中，给定策略集H、观测支持O和目标量τ，测试O是否能分离所有具有不同τ的策略对。在受控的求解器驱动环境中实施，审计无需模型调用，通过检查观测支持是否在其他策略对之间保持区分能力来验证。

Result: 基础观测将7个冻结确定性策略合并为1个等价类；完整支持产生7个类别且无跨目标量碰撞；每个留一支持都保留了构造性碰撞见证。经验上两个约束生成变体的配对有效性均为1.0，但基础准确率与选择性响应保真度显著分歧（0.620 vs 0.324，置信区间不重叠），且在第二个确定性源上再现（0.646 vs 0.331）。审计合成了最小识别支持O*（2格而非36格）。

Conclusion: 评估设计有效性可以在模型推理前从结构上检验，基础正确性不能决定干预-响应保真度。协议级可辨识性审计为LLM基准的评估设计提供了零成本的先验验证手段。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [63] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现，当提示词中包含更常被女性使用的语言特征（如模糊限制语、反义疑问句、集体指称）时，LLM会系统地生成更短、更不精细、更不正式的回答。这些效应在三种文档类型和四种模型中持续存在，且影响远超显性性别线索（如署名），并且事后缓解（post-hoc mitigation）颇为困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业沟通越来越多地由LLM介导，但需要探究这些模型是否对所有用户公平服务。此前研究多关注显性性别偏见，但对语言变体（linguistic variation）这种隐性且文化嵌入的特征关注不足。

Method: 研究者构造包含女性常用语言特征（hedges、tag questions、collective reference）的提示词，跨三种文档类型和四种LLM进行系统性测试，同时控制提示复杂度与特征遗留影响；对比显性性别线索（署名）与隐性语言特征的效果；并通过机制分析探究这些特征的编码位置与纠缠程度。

Result: 语言特征（女性常用语体）系统性导致更短、更不精细、更不正式的输出，效果在所有模型和文档类型中一致；显性性别署名无影响，但语言语域影响巨大，且两者共享表示空间；事后缓解困难，特征在Transformer早期层编码且与其他特征纠缠。

Conclusion: LLM对语言变体存在系统性的隐性偏见，这种偏见文化嵌入、超出用户意识控制且难以事后修正。呼吁在模型开发的上游阶段就考虑语言变异的影响，以减轻LLM介导职场沟通中的不公平影响。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [64] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RippleMem是一种长期记忆系统，通过自适应联想回忆替代一次性检索，在事件中心记忆图中沿语义和结构关联扩展记忆锚点，以恢复缺失的支持性证据，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上取得最佳性能，同时将图构建成本降低约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部记忆进行长时程推理，但关键在于恢复跨多次交互分布的、正确的证据集。现有方法存在瓶颈：全上下文方法需要嘈杂的长上下文搜索，平面检索常返回孤立且不完整的记录，而基于图的记忆系统构建成本高昂且会压缩丰富的事件上下文。

Method: RippleMem将交互历史存储为富含线索的情景记忆单元，并组织成事件中心的记忆图。查询时首先通过混合线索回忆相关记忆锚点，然后沿着语义和结构关联从锚点扩展，以恢复缺失的支持性证据。初始回忆的记忆不仅作为答案上下文，还作为完成证据检索的线索。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，RippleMem在所有评估设置中取得最佳总体性能：LoCoMo上LLM-as-a-Judge准确率提升3.95%，LongMemEval-S上提升高达11.87%，同时图构建成本降低约30倍。

Conclusion: RippleMem验证了联想式回忆范式在长时程记忆系统中的有效性，表明将检索到的记忆作为后续证据补全的线索，能显著提升证据恢复的完整性和准确性，同时大幅降低记忆图构建的计算开销。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [65] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CROP（Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation），一种基于反事实敏感性和释义校准的令牌级选择性蒸馏方法。它通过衡量响应位置对任务相关条件变化的敏感性（并由保持语义不变的改写敏感性校准），来识别更有训练价值的监督位置。实验表明，CROP在两种师生设置下分别比最强非CROP选择器提升1.92和2.96个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的选择性在线策略蒸馏（Selective OPD）方法在分配令牌级监督时主要关注优化需求（如不确定性和师生分歧），而忽略了任务相关性这一维度——即监督是否与当前输入的语义内容紧密相关。作者希望补充这一缺失维度，以更全面地识别有价值的训练信号。

Method: CROP通过三步实现：1) 为每个源提示构建经过验证的"原始-释义-反事实"三元组；2) 固定学生模型的rollout不变；3) 对每个响应位置，测量其对任务相关条件变化的敏感性，并用对保持语义的改写（释义）的敏感性进行校准，得到最终的任务相关性得分。这种设计使CROP成为模型内部（model-internal）且对比特定（contrast-specific）的令牌级监督分配方法。

Result: 匹配的选择控制实验表明，CROP识别的监督位置优于随机选择或最低相关性选择；组件对比实验证实了反事实敏感性和释义校准两者的价值。在两种师生设置下，CROP相比最强非CROP选择器分别获得1.92和2.96个百分点的整体性能提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持任务相关性可作为选择性OPD的补充标准，并确立了CROP作为一种有效的模型内部、对比特定的令牌级监督分配方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [66] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对九个自监督语音骨干模型进行了分层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针在三种语言上评估帕金森病检测。结果显示：最优表征层主要由源数据集决定而非架构本身；迁移的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，模型对帕金森病和痴呆语音都赋予高概率。这些发现揭示了语音病理识别模型在临床部署前的关键局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音表征在单一语料库内对帕金森病检测表现出色，但尚不清楚模型是捕捉了疾病相关特征还是利用了数据集特定的混杂因素。由于大多数SSL骨干仅在健康语音上预训练，这一不确定性亟需澄清，以确保模型的可信度。

Method: 对九个SSL语音骨干进行逐层（layer-wise）分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针，覆盖三种语言。评估被构建为多个场景，逐步引入参与者身份、录音条件、语言和病理类型的分布偏移。

Result: 两个关键发现：(1) 层选择高度依赖语料库——最优表征层主要由源数据集决定而非SSL架构本身；(2) 迁移的判别信号缺乏病理特异性——训练用于检测帕金森病的分类器对目标语料库中的帕金森病语音和痴呆语音都赋予相似的高概率。

Conclusion: 当前SSL语音表征在跨语料库迁移时存在严重局限，既缺乏泛化性也缺乏病理特异性。在语音病理识别模型被可靠地应用于临床之前，这些问题必须得到解决。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [67] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究指令微调对语言模型在问答任务中置信度与理由（rationale）词法多样性的影响，发现指令微调一致性地改变模型置信度，但跨理由多样性持续下降而表面词法多样性变化方向不定，且二者是独立效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型在生成任务上表现优异，但会表现出口头表达的过度自信。已有研究表明，问答中模型的口头过度自信可能与生成理由的一致性相关，因此需要探究指令微调是否通过改变理由的词法多样性来影响模型置信度。

Method: 选取三组匹配的基座模型与指令微调模型，在多个问答基准上评估模型置信度变化、预测准确率、基于似然的校准，并分析理由的跨理由多样性与表面词法多样性，同时控制答案选择与理由长度等因素进行验证。

Result: 指令微调一致性地改变模型置信度，但预测准确率变化有限且似然校准有所下降；跨理由多样性一致下降，而表面词法多样性在不同模型和基准上方向和幅度均不一致；在控制答案选择和理由长度后，这些差异依然存在。

Conclusion: 置信度与理由多样性反映的是指令微调的不同独立效应，指令微调对模型口头自信的影响并非简单通过词法多样性变化来传导。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [68] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从格赖斯（Grice）语用学视角审视LLM的幻觉问题：当被问及知识边界外的实体时，LLM倾向于编造看似合理的具体细节，而非退回到更安全、更笼统的表述。研究发现LLM的激活中确实编码了"实体是否在知识边界内"以及"即将生成的指称的具体性"两个信号，但这两个信号在生成过程中未被调和——模型即使知道实体未知，仍偏向生成具体的指称。这意味着格赖斯式"撤退"行为所需的基础要素已存在，但缺乏相应的生成策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在知识边界外会产生幻觉（编造看似合理的细节），作者希望从语用学（Gricean合作原则）角度理解这一失败——一个合作的说话者在不确定指称对象时，应当从具体性层级上"撤退"，牺牲信息量以换取真实性。核心问题是：LLM是否具备这种撤退的能力要素？

Method: 基于T-REx构建基准测试，操纵实体熟悉度和指称具体性两个维度。通过探针（probing）实验回答两个问题：（i）模型激活是否编码了指称是否在知识边界内的信号；（ii）模型是否能预判即将生成的指称的具体性。

Result: 两个问题的答案都是"是"——模型确实编码了知识边界信号和指称具体性预期信号。但关键发现是：这两个信号在生成时没有被调和。模型即使面对未知实体，仍强烈偏向生成具体指称，甚至在提供正确泛化替代选项的情况下也是如此。

Conclusion: LLM具备格赖斯式撤退的底层基础（知识边界感知和具体性预判），但缺乏将两者结合起来的生成策略。这为"格赖斯对齐"（Gricean alignment）提供了方向：训练或引导目标应耦合知识边界感知与指称具体性，使模型在不确定时主动退回到更安全的一般性表述。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需下游任务或验证集作为归因目标的语言模型预训练数据影响力度量方法，通过衡量梯度更新与最终参数距离的平方缩减来定义样例影响力，并由中间检查点估计该量。在Pythia和PolyPythia的18种配置上发现早期文学相关数据与最终参数轨迹更对齐，后期STEM数据更对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言模型预训练测量训练数据影响力具有挑战性：难以选择能代表模型通用能力的下游任务或验证集，且依赖中间检查点任务性能的比较方式复杂化了跨训练比较。

Method: 将某个样例的影响力定义为：其梯度更新使到最终参数平方距离减少的程度；该量无需重新训练即可从中间检查点估计。

Result: 应用于Pythia和PolyPythia套件的18种配置，发现影响力的系统时序变化：早期文献相关数据与最终参数轨迹更对齐，后期STEM数据对齐更强，且该定性交叉在不同模型配置间一致。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可操作的轨迹级视角来观察预训练过程中有影响力数据的动态变化，补充了针对特定下游任务或验证集定义的影响力分析方法。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [70] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mimir v1，一个拥有10亿参数的开放许可语言模型，基于层次推理模型（HRM）架构从零训练，仅使用许可数据即可在英语上取得有竞争力的性能，并在丹麦语上达到新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型开发依赖于海量且常不合规的数据集，这为致力于开源和符合伦理数据的研究人员设置了极高的门槛。作者希望降低这一障碍，证明使用纯许可数据也能训练出高性能的模型。

Method: 基于HRM（分层推理模型）架构构建1B参数语言模型，使用161个数据集的混合数据进行从零训练，仅在事后训练（post-training）阶段使用许可数据。

Result: Mimir v1在20个基准测试（英语、数学与代码、丹麦语）中超越了原始的HRM-Text 1B，并能与更大的前沿模型（如Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B）竞争；在丹麦语上达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在仅使用许可数据的前提下，小型模型仍能获得极具竞争力的性能，为开源和数据伦理友好的语言模型研究提供了可行路径。模型已在Hugging Face Hub上公开。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [71] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAEVerbalizer框架，通过将SAE解码器方向注入LLM表示并微调下游层，直接从解码器方向生成自然语言解释，无需外部行为观察，能泛化到未见特征并跨SAE字典和不同LLM迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE特征解释依赖外部观察，导致两个问题：(1) 基于观察到的模型行为得到的解释往往浅薄表面化；(2) 大规模收集行为证据的计算效率低下。需要一种直接从SAE内部结构生成解释的方法。

Method: SAEVerbalizer框架：将SAE解码器方向注入LLM表示中，微调LLM的后续层以生成对注入特征的自然语言解释。训练后的verbalizer直接从解码器方向解释SAE特征，无需外部观察。

Result: 实验表明：(1) verbilization能力可泛化到未见特征；(2) 可跨训练好的SAE字典迁移；(3) 通过轻量适配器可扩展到不同LLM的SAE特征。干预实验显示：注入多个方向会产生组合含义的解释，反转单个方向会产生相应的语义转移。

Conclusion: SAEVerbalizer能直接从SAE解码器方向生成自然语言解释，解决了现有方法依赖外部观察导致的浅层解释和低效率问题，并具有良好的泛化性和迁移性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [72] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LITTLECURRICULUM，一个专门针对美国小学课程（5年级及以下）的88B token预训练语料库，并用其训练出LITTLELEARNER模型，作为研究模型知识获取与能力边界的受控沙箱环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型在异质的网络规模文本上训练，难以研究模型的知识和技能获取过程，因为无法精确刻画模型先前接触过的相关内容。

Method: 构建一个8B8B token的预训练语料库LITTLECURRICULUM，明确排除5年级以上教授的概念、事实和词汇；从零开始训练一个5B参数的LLM得到LITTLELEARNER模型，并将其作为受控环境研究模型的知识获取、表示和使用。

Result: LITTLELEARNER具备足够的语言能力进行开放式评估，但具有清晰的知识和能力边界。通过后训练和上下文学习注入新知识，模型能够更好地利用已有知识，但不会提升超出范围的能力。

Conclusion: 该受控沙箱环境对于未来研究模型如何获取、表示和使用数据具有重要价值，为可解释的课程指南映射到模型能力边界提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

### [73] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将预训练黑盒预测函数的知识迁移到异构输入空间的方法，通过将目标回归函数分解为可迁移和不可迁移两部分，利用无标签特征对和有限标签进行两步神经网络估计，并给出了优于非迁移方法的预测风险界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑盒模型蕴含大量数据知识，但当可用输入特征与黑盒期望的特征不一致时无法直接使用。现有方法缺乏在异构输入空间间迁移预测知识的系统性方案，需要解决特征不匹配下的知识迁移问题。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为可迁移组件（由黑盒提供信息）和不可迁移组件（捕捉新空间的独特信息）。采用两步神经网络流程：第一步从丰富的无标签特征对中估计可迁移组件，第二步从有限标签中估计不可迁移组件。框架可扩展至多个黑盒并支持聚合。

Result: 推导了预测风险界，在不可迁移组件较小或平滑时优于非迁移方法，且过程能自适应两种情况。在附加条件下，估计量的最坏情况风险在多项式阶上严格小于仅用标注数据的最小最大风险。模拟和真实数据验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了从预训练黑盒到异构输入空间的知识迁移，通过分解可迁移与不可迁移组件实现高效学习，可扩展到多黑盒聚合场景，在理论上和实践上均有显著价值。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [74] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SSPO（结构感知相似度加权偏好优化）方法，通过不相似度加权的留一法基线联合评分所有B个采样解，解决神经组合优化中梯度信号极化和基线冗余两大问题，并在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上取得一致提升，且已在京东生产系统中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCO训练方法未能充分利用共采样解组中的丰富信息：偏好优化方法锚定单最优解并丢弃其他解的细粒度质量和结构信号（梯度信号极化）；均值基线均匀加权导致结构相似的解淹没基线并保持高梯度方差（基线冗余）。

Method: 提出SSPO，通过不相似度加权的留一法基线联合评分所有B个采样解：结构不同的解获得更高权重，单一机制同时解决两个失败。基线使用零参数、问题自适应的解嵌入，由编码器现有的节点表示构建。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上相比此前最优锚定和均匀权重基线取得一致提升；与均匀RLOO的直接对比证实结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动力；SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东生产设施选址系统中部署。

Conclusion: 结构感知的加权机制能更充分利用共采样解组中的信息，提升NCO训练效果，并具备实际生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [75] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题。在生成模型假设下，建立了同步和异步QTD的功能中心极限定理，并提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，该方法利用QTD路径信息构造渐近枢轴统计量，无需存储整个轨迹，大幅降低内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中的QTD算法虽然被广泛应用，但其统计推断（如置信区间构建、假设检验）缺乏理论基础。现有的QTD推断方法要么需要存储整个迭代轨迹导致内存开销大，要么缺乏严格的统计保证，因此需要开发既有理论保障又内存高效的统计推断方法。

Method: 1) 在生成模型可访问的假设下，建立同步和异步QTD迭代平均值的功能中心极限定理，证明其弱收敛到重标度的布朗运动；2) 提出基于随机缩放的在线统计推断方法，构造渐近枢轴统计量用于推断，该统计量利用整个QTD路径的信息，但可通过在线方式计算，无需存储完整轨迹。

Result: 建立了QTD的功能中心极限定理（同步和异步），证明迭代平均值弱收敛于重标度布朗运动；提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，构造了渐近枢轴统计量，显著降低了内存需求，实现了分布强化学习中高效的统计推断。

Conclusion: 该研究为分布强化学习中的QTD算法提供了坚实的统计推断理论基础，所提出的在线推断方法在保证统计有效性的同时大幅降低了内存开销，为实际应用中QTD的可靠性评估和不确定性量化提供了可行工具。
