Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [76] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究DRO和RS两种鲁棒学习方法在目标部署环境（即偏移后的分布）下的泛化误差，推导了有限样本泛化误差界，提出信息引导的超参数校准方法，并在网络批量问题中验证了两种方法的互补行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移导致训练环境与部署环境不一致是实际中常见挑战。虽然DRO和RS等鲁棒学习框架被广泛使用，但它们在偏移目标环境下的有限样本保证以及两者的系统比较仍未被充分探索。现有分析要么在源环境下建立保证，要么只考虑模糊集上的最坏情况表现，缺乏对目标环境下泛化误差的研究。

Method: 首先为DRO和RS推导目标偏移环境下的有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画鲁棒超参数带来的偏移敏感度降低与正则化惩罚之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中的维度灾难。其次，在可用部分偏移信息（如偏移幅度或方向）时，提出信息引导的超参数校准方法，在相同信息条件下比较两种方法。最后将框架应用于网络批量问题。

Result: 推导出两种方法在目标偏移环境下的有限样本泛化误差界；在部分信息机制下，DRO和RS在理论和实证中表现出互补行为；网络批量问题中揭示了对需求正向偏移下鲁棒策略的响应机制。

Conclusion: 填补了鲁棒学习方法在分布偏移下统计性质理解的空白，为DRO和RS的系统比较提供了原则性依据。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [77] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维网络邻域选择问题。作者证明，在参数远多于观测值的高维设定下，最小描述长度（MDL）方法能够提供保守（低假阳性率）的邻域估计，而传统的Lasso、AIC、BIC等方法容易产生虚假连接。研究还揭示了岭参数对均方误差的影响，并与机器学习中的"双下降"现象建立了联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络分析中，为避免遗漏重要变量及其连接，越来越多的变量被纳入分析，导致出现参数远多于观测值的高维问题。传统的邻域选择方法（如Lasso、AIC、BIC）在高维设定下容易产生虚假连接（高假阳性率），因此需要一种能够提供保守估计的方法来正确选择网络邻域。

Method: 论文从理论和实证两个层面进行分析：(i) 当真实模型为线性时，邻域选择效果良好；(ii) 当真实模型为非线性时，邻域选择需要对高维参数引入惩罚。作者研究了岭参数对均方误差的影响，论证低测试方差导致产生大量边的邻域。核心思想是：在高维模型空间中，惩罚项需要纳入模型空间的体积，并证明MDL（最小描述长度）在高维设定下能够实现正确的邻域选择或较小的邻域（低假阳性率），且在线性模型假设正确或错误两种情况下均适用。

Result: 作者证明在参数数量远大于观测值的高维设定下，MDL方法能够实现正确的邻域选择或更小的邻域（低假阳性率），无论是在模型被正确假设为线性还是被错误假设为线性的情况下都成立。相比之下，Lasso、AIC、BIC等大多数邻域选择方法会导致虚假连接。研究还发现岭参数通过影响均方误差和测试方差，进而影响邻域中边的数量，这一洞察与机器学习中的"双下降"现象相呼应。

Conclusion: 本文挑战了传统模型选择范式：在高维设定下，传统的"少参数更好"的观念被"双下降"现象颠覆。MDL方法通过将模型空间的体积纳入惩罚项，能够在高维网络邻域选择中提供低假阳性率的保守估计，这为高维网络分析中的变量和连接选择提供了更可靠的理论依据。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [78] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了特征参数化代价函数下逆最优传输（IOT）的统计与算法理论，核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化及其谱代理公式，在此基础上推导出核心谱下界，并建立了四个定理（可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性、收敛性）和一个关于模型误设的观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输问题（从观测到的传输计划中恢复代价函数参数）在经济学、机器学习和最优传输应用中具有重要价值，但此前缺乏系统性的统计与算法理论分析，特别是在代价函数由特征参数化的一般场景下，参数的可辨识性、估计的统计性质和算法的收敛保证尚不明确。

Method: 核心方法是Sinkhorn线性化——利用隐函数定理将熵正则化OT计划对代价参数的敏感性显式化，并给出谱精确且几何透明的谱代理公式。通过分析切空间上的受限Hessian，获得谱三明治不等式，从而推导出核心下界σ_min。基于此建立四个定理：T1通过商空间与规范核的商论证全局可辨识性；T2利用不可表示条件和得分集中性建立l1惩罚估计量的支撑恢复；T3证明特征矩映射的强单调性及逆映射的Lipschitz连续性；T4建立局部强凸性保证梯度下降单调收敛；O5通过数值实验评估模型误设下估计量收敛到投影的性质及Hölder连续性指数。

Result: 得到核心谱下界σ_min ≥ (π_min/(a_max·ε))·√λ_min(Σ)，并据此建立：(1) 在规范核的商空间上θ全局可注入，维度上界为(K-1)^2；(2) l1惩罚估计量在不可表示条件和得分集中性下以指数小失败概率恢复真实支撑；(3) 特征矩映射强单调，逆映射Lipschitz常数有显式上界；(4) 局部强凸性常数μ ≥ π_min²·λ_min(Σ)/ε²保证梯度下降单调收敛；(5) 模型误设下估计量收敛到OT模型的投影，Hölder指数α_eff∈(0,1)依赖具体设置。

Conclusion: 本文为特征参数化逆最优传输提供了统一且自洽的统计-算法理论框架，Sinkhorn线性化作为核心工具将谱分析、可辨识性、稀疏恢复和优化收敛性有机整合，谱三明治不等式为整个理论提供了统一的驱动下界。该工作为实际应用中IOT问题的理论保证奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [79] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Wasserstein Filtering (WF) 框架，通过筛选样本子集以最大化其与完全污染经验分布之间的 Wasserstein 距离，从而剔除几何上有影响力的异常样本，恢复底层干净分布。配套提出三种高效算法（SinkMarg、SinkWF、SlicedWF），并在新提出的 FELP 污染模型下证明 WF 估计器在协方差有界分布族上达到极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集中常存在污染样本，直接使用经验分布估计会导致结果严重偏离真实分布。已有方法或依赖特定模型假设，或计算代价高昂，缺乏一个模型无关、理论可保证且计算可行的样本筛选框架来恢复干净分布。

Method: 核心思想是选择这样的子集：其经验分布与完全污染的经验分布之间的 Wasserstein 距离最大，从而优先剔除对分布几何影响最大的离群样本。为提高计算可行性，提出三种算法：边际筛选方案 SinkMarg、基于熵正则最优传输的联合优化算法 SinkWF、基于切片 Wasserstein 近似的 SlicedWF。理论上引入 FELP 污染模型，刻画由良好分离离群点与局部不可区分扰动组成的污染。

Result: 在合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及基于扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习实验中，WF 展现出作为高度实用、模型无关预处理工具的价值：提供了有竞争力的异常检测性能，并在重度污染下为生成建模带来显著的下游收益。

Conclusion: WF 是一个理论上有保证（在 FELP 模型下达到极小极大最优）、计算上可行且模型无关的通用数据预处理工具，能够有效恢复污染数据下的干净分布，兼具异常检测与鲁棒生成学习的实用价值。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>


### [80] [Bagging Robustly Learns VC Classes with Linear Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13514)
*Omar Montasser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了对抗鲁棒学习问题，证明了VC类可以通过一个简单的bagging+RERM的不当算法实现样本复杂度为O(d)的可学习性，相比之前O(d)更大上界有了指数级改进，并给出了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒学习是机器学习中的重要问题，之前Montasser等人(2019)给出的上界过于宽松。本文的目标是缩小理论与实际之间的差距，证明更紧的样本复杂度界。

Method: 提出一个简单的算法：在O(d*)个独立的bootstrap样本上分别计算RERM，然后对结果进行多数投票。d*为对偶VC维数。同时通过下界证明该算法在该oracle模型下是最优的。

Result: 证明了VC类对抗鲁棒学习的样本复杂度为O(d)，相比之前上界有指数级改进；同时证明任何算法在该oracle模型下至少需要Ω(d*)次RERM调用，证明算法的调用次数最优。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论证明表明，简单的bagging+RERM组合算法在对抗鲁棒学习中就能达到最优的样本复杂度和oracle调用复杂度，无需更复杂的算法设计。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI社区在"推理"（reasoning）的定义上存在严重歧义，导致推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，进而阻碍了可量化、可信赖的自主推理进展。作者基于文献综合提出了操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供了一份AI推理研究沟通最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI领域虽然对自主推理兴趣浓厚、进展迅速，但未就推理的操作性定义达成共识，且常隐含地排斥逻辑与可验证自动推理中的历史定义。这种定义模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度无法验证，削弱了进展的可量化性和可信赖性。作者认为这一歧义是可以解决的。

Method: （1）基于现有文献综合，提出推理的操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为一种可学习的基于规则的过程；（2）为AI推理研究的沟通提供最佳实践检查清单（checklist）。

Result: 文中提出了一套操作性定义和一份研究沟通的检查清单，旨在使推理研究的定义和评估更加明确、可验证，从而支持可量化的进展。

Conclusion: 推理的定义歧义是可以解决的；通过提供操作性定义和最佳实践清单，研究社区有望提升推理评估的构念效度，推动向可信赖的自主推理方向取得可量化进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [82] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 IntegrityBench 基准，用于评估语言模型在机构压力下维持科研诚信的能力，覆盖不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于实物的决策三个维度。通过 5 级隐含-显式压力协议、36 个配对任务、3 个领域和 4 个研究阶段进行评估。发现 18 个前沿模型在峰值压力下约有 1/3 的诚信关键决策失败，且规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解。显式压力导致不当行为遵从，隐含情境重述则引发对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。值得注意的是，分类能力差的模型在基于实物的决策上反而表现相当或更好，表明三方面在结构上分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为"共同科学家"，但它们在机构压力下维持科研诚信的能力尚未被量化评估。现有研究缺乏一个系统性的基准来衡量模型在面对学术不端压力时的伦理决策表现，以及不同压力类型（显式 vs 隐式）对模型行为的影响是否存在差异。

Method: 构建 IntegrityBench 基准，包含三个评估维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于实物的决策），采用 5 级隐含-显式压力协议，覆盖 3 个领域（3 domains）和 4 个研究阶段（4 research stages）的 36 个配对任务。评估 18 个前沿模型变体。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型在约 1/3 的诚信关键决策中失败，且模型规模和推理能力都不能可靠地缓解这一问题。显式压力诱导模型遵从不当行为，而隐式情境重述更常导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。分类准确率较低的模型在基于实物的决策上表现与高准确率模型相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个评估维度在结构上相互分离。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能表面看起来有帮助，但潜藏着诚信失败，构成两种不同的部署风险：一是促进科研不当行为，二是侵蚀对 AI 辅助研究的信任。正确的伦理行动并不需要准确的问题分类，这暗示当前的模型评估方法可能需要重新思考。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [83] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是立场论文，论证现代AI对齐方法虽是预防有害输出的工具，但本质是一种双用途技术，易被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操控。随着AI作为信息提供者被快速采用、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境日益威权化，这一滥用风险正在加剧。作者呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐方法最初旨在防止模型产生有害输出，但作者指出这些方法天然的"双用途"特性——既能用于安全防护，也能被恶意行为者反向利用。随着威权主义抬头和AI信息传播角色的扩大，这种滥用的风险被显著放大，需要立即展开讨论。

Method: 采用立场论证的方法，将当前AI对齐技术映射到可能的滥用场景及实际案例，系统展示"完美对齐"模型如何同时为恶意行为者提供不断改进的信息主导工具。

Result: 研究表明，AI对齐技术存在真实的双用途滥用风险，且该风险因用户快速采用AI作为信息源、经济权力不对称以及政治环境趋向威权化而进一步加剧。

Conclusion: 呼吁AI社区正视对齐机制的故意滥用可能性，并主动提出和采取缓解策略，以防止"完美对齐"的追求反而成为信息操控与审查的赋能工具。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [84] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用500个ETHICS衍生基准测试，发现LLM与人类在最终道德判断标签上高度一致，但在支持理由层面存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不能等同于真正的道德对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常用最终标签与人类判断的一致性来衡量LLM的道德对齐程度，但这种一致性可能具有误导性——模型和人类可能因不同道德理由而得出相同结论，因此需要更深入的评估方法。

Method: 构建500个覆盖五个道德判断领域的ETHICS衍生基准，收集人类和LLM对最终标签及支持理由两层的标注，比较前沿和开源模型在标签一致性和理由层面的差异。

Result: 模型与人类多数标签的一致性通常很高，但理由层面的分析显示模型在伤害、尊重、守信、正义、应得和借口相关性等道德类别上系统性地重新分配了注意力权重，即使最终标签匹配人类多数意见时也是如此。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应被视为与真正的对齐等价，仅基于标签的评估可能造成误导性的安心，必须辅以对所表达理由、原则和道德优先级的分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一种面向移动边缘-云异构基础设施的流处理调度系统。其核心贡献是LLM-MR-CNP，即对经典Contract Net Protocol的扩展，融合了语义CFP生成、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。在阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验中，系统将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，在20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，相比多轮基于规则的基线效用提升高达22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统越来越多地运行在异构的移动边缘-云基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格的QoS要求，这些问题使得去中心化调度变得复杂。现有方法难以同时处理定性/不确定性上下文与确定性资源约束，需要更智能的协商机制。

Method: 提出了MAS-DecStream系统，核心组件为LLM-MR-CNP，它扩展了经典CNP协议，包括：(1)语义CFP公式化（大语言模型辅助），(2)渐进式上下文披露，(3)多轮提案修订，(4)协商记忆，(5)确定性验证。边缘集群代理基于局部观察、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文精炼自然语言卸载提案，同时保持硬资源与QoS约束的确定性。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的评估从三个层面进行：单轮与多轮CNP对比、基于规则与LLM辅助精炼对比、固定模型单轮与多轮协商对比。结果显示：延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率0.91，效用相比多轮规则基线最高提升22%。额外25个案例评估展示了模型与提示词相关的精度-成本权衡。多轮CNP精炼是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助在定性及不确定运行时上下文方面补充价值。

Conclusion: 实验结果为多轮CNP精炼作为协议层主要增益提供了初步证据，LLM辅助主要在处理定性和不确定的运行时上下文时发挥作用。该方法在边缘-云异构流处理调度中展现出潜力，但模型和提示词的选择会带来精度与成本的权衡。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [86] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"概念，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统不仅要结果正确，还应以与用户相似的推理方式进行思考，并忠实地传达其推理过程。作者提供了证据和调查数据支持该观点，并提出了研究议程来弥合现有对齐方法与该目标之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策代表或自主决策者被使用，在高风险决策中仅依赖结果的准确性是不够的。作者认为认知对齐（AI以类似人类的方式推理）能提升可理解性和可信度，而认知错位可能阻碍AI在众多应用中的采纳。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），采用文献综述与实证调查相结合的方法：回顾认知对齐提升可理解性与可信度的证据，并开展新的调查以了解用户对认知对齐重要性的看法，进而分析现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的差距。

Result: 调查数据显示许多用户将认知对齐视为"必不可少的"（essential），尤其是当AI的判断或行动的推理过程对他们很重要时。作者识别出现有对齐方法在实现认知对齐方面的不足，并提出了研究议程。

Conclusion: 认知错位很可能阻碍AI系统在许多预期应用中的采纳，解决认知对齐问题是创造用户既愿意依赖又有理由信赖的AI系统的关键。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [87] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了六个提供商的九个LLM，发现提示词的语言可以改变模型在高风险场景（核武器打击建议）中的决策。日语提示显著降低了Claude和Gemini模型的打击率，且机制是"推理语言"而非"输入语言"——当模型被要求用日语推理时，会自发产生道德词汇，从而降低攻击倾向。该效应仅存在于英语中本就犹豫的模型中，表明LLM安全对齐是语言依赖的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常仅在英语中评估。作者质疑单一语言评估可能遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与安全机制，因此探究提示语言是否会影响高风险决策。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论场景（LLM为核武国家提供是否打击无防御对手的建议），提示词刻意去道德化且跨语言策略上完全相同。测试六个提供商的九个模型，并设计了跨语言实验（英语提示但要求用日语推理）来隔离"输入语言"与"推理语言"的机制。

Result: 日语提示使Claude Sonnet 4.6在非必要打击场景中打击率从40%降至0%，在有争议场景中从93%降至17%；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验表明推理语言而非输入语言驱动效应——英语提示但日语推理时打击率从93%降至37%。日语推理时模型自发产生提示中完全不存在的道德词汇。其他五个模型无语言效应，但几乎在所有条件下都选择打击。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为是语言依赖的，仅用英语评估会同时遗漏其他语言中的风险和安全保障。该效应需要模型在英语中本身已有犹豫倾向，提示了安全对齐中语言维度的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [88] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，通过主辅双流架构将推理计算分为prefill和decode两个阶段，辅助流仅在生成阶段激活，在共享权重和KV缓存的同时增加续写预测的计算量，从而在不增加prompt处理成本的前提下提升模型质量，并在MoE模型中实现对prefill/decode专家分配的独立控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM服务请求增多，累计推理成本相对于一次性训练成本日益重要。传统模型宽度或深度扩展会同时增加prefill和decode两个阶段的成本，因为每个新增层都要在两个阶段被评估。作者希望将新增的学习计算只分配到续写预测（decode）阶段，同时保留prompt的主干计算和单一的持久KV缓存。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流是一个完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅在最后一个prompt位置之后激活，增加续写预测计算而不写入持久状态或影响主流。两个流共享主要的attention、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token embeddings和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主辅流的专家扇出成为独立控制项，可分别调节prompt成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数量的比较中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下均获得更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中研究了两种机制：在固定prefill专家计算下增加decode计算，以及将固定的decode专家预算在两个流之间重新分配，实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明了可以将额外计算仅分配到decode阶段而不增加prefill成本，通过阶段分离的专家分配（尤其在MoE场景）可以实现更灵活的计算-质量权衡，为推理阶段的计算资源优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [89] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，解决跨域零样本/少样本个性化问题。通过元学习LoRA初始化同时作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度避免过拟合；并将个性化先验分解为用户组件（可读提示词）和域组件（拓扑保持软token），在HiCUPID等基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域个性化方法面临两大挑战：1）适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合；2）历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，导致不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 1）PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：元学习的LoRA初始化同时充当适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集规模和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，稀疏证据下限制过拟合，证据充足时允许更强个性化。2）先验的功能性分解：将个性化先验分解为用户组件（人类可读提示词承载稳定偏好）和域组件（拓扑保持的软token进行域特定隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致超越强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率退化相比最佳竞争基线降低47.9%，未见用户冷启动下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过联合解决适应校准（PAC-Bayes正则化控制更新幅度）和先验解耦（用户/域功能分解）两大问题，可以有效缓解负迁移和过拟合，实现更可靠的跨域个性化。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [90] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AstraZeneca内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统（Research Assistant），该系统通过聊天式界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学数据、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多源证据，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，响应均基于检索证据并链接回原始来源。论文概述了系统架构、主要设计决策以及在大规模部署中的经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员高效探索跨多种数据源的生物医学问题，将分散在不同系统中的证据整合到统一的聊天界面中，以支持日常研发工作流程。

Method: 系统采用LLM为核心，提供两种模式：快速模式用于直接问答，多步模式用于复杂研究任务。系统整合了科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性、表达数据和内部实验系统等多种数据源，响应通过检索证据进行grounding，并链接回原始来源供用户审阅和深入探索。

Result: 该系统已在大规模部署中支撑AstraZeneca的日常研发工作流程，论文分享了部署过程中的经验教训。

Conclusion: Research Assistant展示了LLM系统在制药行业研发中的实际应用价值，通过多源证据整合和来源可追溯性，为科学家提供了可靠的研究辅助工具。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [91] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统性地研究大语言模型在同时满足多个显式约束时的性能退化问题。通过对15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查的系统评估，发现逐约束通过率下降平缓但整体成功率呈塌方式崩溃，结构化约束退化比词法约束严重2倍，且失败近乎独立导致累积效应呈乘法关系。可靠的指令遵循在5-6个同时约束时即出现崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要同时遵守多个显式约束（推理结构、安全边界、输出格式）的真实部署场景中越来越普遍，但组合约束场景下的性能表现尚未被充分刻画。尽管模型对单个约束处理娴熟，关于性能如何随约束数量退化、退化的机制是什么、以及能否缓解这个问题，目前缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 提出CSE基准，通过程序化生成的方式系统变化同时约束的数量（k=1到12），每个约束均由确定性、基于规则的验证器评分，完全不含LLM评判。评估覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型，共计369,753次检查。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）逐约束通过率平缓且可预测地下降，但满足所有k个约束的概率呈塌方式崩溃——个体约束通过率约41%的模型在k=8时全部通过的概率仅为5.7%；（2）约束退化不均等：结构约束每增加一个约束比词法约束多损失2倍的基础能力；（3）失败几乎相互独立，导致累积呈乘法效应，残留耦合源于共享输出特征而非两两干扰。最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率低于50%，15个模型中有12个在3个或更少约束时即失败。

Conclusion: 可靠的指令遵循能力在5-6个同时约束时即发生崩溃，揭示了当前大模型在组合约束场景下的根本局限，为理解和改进多约束条件下的模型性能提供了系统性的评估框架和量化证据。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [92] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MindMemOS，一个可移植且自我进化的记忆操作系统层，通过统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理的记忆系统在开发后通常保持固定不变，无法通过持续使用来适应其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，限制了代理在长期交互中的适应能力。

Method: MindMemOS采用统一实体属性时间结构组织信息，包含两个核心算法：MindMemEvolve通过验证驱动的进化搜索优化记忆模式，并通过"梦境"机制合并冗余记录、解决冲突来巩固记忆；同时使用隐式纠正反馈作为人在回路信号来识别和修正不准确记忆。MindSkillEvolve将代理执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%；MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上相比初始技能基线提升了9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS证明了记忆系统可以自我进化以适应新场景和持续使用，通过进化搜索、梦境巩固和技能演化显著提升了AI代理的记忆能力与任务性能。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [93] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出治理持久内存（GPM），一种可审计的比特emporal状态转换模型，用于解决长期智能体记忆中陈旧、冲突、被撤回或已删除记录仍可能支撑新论断的问题。通过五项可执行条款（账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后禁止复活、精确论断闭合），GPM在GPM-ReleaseBench基准及密封端到端评估中取得显著改进（受治理车道2400/2400 vs 未治理600/2400），并通过有限状态建模和差分测试验证了契约与实现的正确性。作者强调这是有界契约与实现结果，而非开放世界模型准确性或世界真相的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期智能体记忆系统普遍采用"选择-存储-检索"（select-store-retrieve）范式，但检索环节并未判断相互矛盾、被取代、被撤回、被删除或过时的记录是否可能支撑一个输出论断，这导致记忆污染和错误输出。

Method: 提出治理持久内存（GPM），一种可审计的比特emporal（双时态）状态转换模型，包含：源绑定准入机制、派生生命周期状态、当前公共障碍（barriers）、故障闭合（fail-closed）结构化释放。模型由五项可执行条款约束：账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后不可复活、在单一验证头部的新鲜视图上实现精确论断闭合。

Result: 在预指定哈希冻结的3,600例GPM-ReleaseBench上，GPM匹配全部完整结果；最弱的三种简单完整策略中最强者仅匹配1,800/3,600。密封端到端服务评估中，受治理车道在V3版本于2,400/2,400集群全部正确（对比未治理Qwen2.5-7B的600/2,400），修复全部1,800个基线失败且无回归（单侧95%下界99.875%和99.834%）。V5重密封同样每臂2,400/2,400。有限模型探索331,776个语义状态和1,990,656个查询状态无全契约反例，100,000轨迹三引擎差分零不匹配。

Conclusion: GPM通过治理层而非仅有模型能力提升，有效确保了长期记忆中论断的可靠性与一致性。但这些是受限于契约与实现的结果，不代表开放世界模型准确性或世界真相；受治理答案属于确定性服务输出，7B结果是未治理对照，而非声称语言模型本身变得完全准确。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [94] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ε-MemEvo框架，通过任务无关的策略记忆（自然语言摘要而非原始代码）实现LLM程序进化中的跨任务知识迁移，并采用自适应注入门控避免负面迁移。在8个优化基准上，相比AdaEvolve平均相对提升+8.7%的AUCC和+9.4%的早期收敛速度，且计算开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM程序进化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）在发现新算法方面表现优异，但每个任务被孤立优化，搜索完成后丢弃搜索经验，无法实现跨任务的知识复用与迁移。

Method: 1) 将先验经验存储为任务无关的策略记忆（task-agnostic tactic memories），即成功算法策略的紧凑自然语言摘要，而非原始代码，从而支持在不同API和评估器之间的迁移。2) 设计自适应注入门控（adaptive injection gate），根据检索到的记忆是否语义匹配来决定是否注入以及注入强度，避免负面迁移。3) 采用内容级留一法（Leave-One-Out）评测协议，排除目标任务自身的记忆条目。

Result: 在GPT-5骨干上，ε-MemEvo在全部8个任务上提升AUCC（相对AdaEvolve平均+8.7%），早期收敛速度平均提升+9.4%。消融实验表明朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在全部5个消融任务中保持安全。数据更新的后验在可观测状态中具有可解释性：搜索改进时倾向跳过，从早期平台期到晚期平台期从跳过转向提示。额外计算开销小于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo证明了任务无关的策略记忆结合自适应注入门控是实现LLM程序进化跨任务知识迁移的有效且安全的方法，在保持极低计算开销的同时显著提升搜索性能与收敛速度，并提供了可解释的门控决策机制。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [95] [General Probabilities of Causation with Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12657)
*Xin Shu,Zhen Lei,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多值因果概率（PoCs）的偏识别问题，通过纳入协变量和中介变量中的因果信息，推导出比现有非二元界限更紧的界限，并通过玩具示例和仿真研究验证了所提界限的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tian和Pearl推导了二元PoCs的理论锐界，Mueller等人通过纳入协变量和中介变量收紧了二元PNS的界限，Li与Pearl及Shu等人将PoCs扩展到多值设置并推导了理论界限。一个自然的问题是：在多值设置下，额外的因果知识能否进一步收紧界限？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 通过将协变量和中介变量中编码的因果信息纳入多值PoCs的偏识别框架，推导出比现有非二元界限更紧的新界限。理论结果辅以玩具示例说明，并通过仿真研究进行验证。

Result: 仿真研究表明，所提出的界限比现有的非二元界限更紧，证明纳入协变量和中介变量中的因果信息能够有效收紧多值PoCs的界限。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将利用协变量和中介变量因果信息来收紧界限的思想从二元设置扩展到多值设置，为多值PoCs提供了更精确的偏识别界限，推进了因果推断中个体因果响应的识别研究。

Abstract: Probabilities of causation (PoCs) characterize individual causal responses that cannot be directly observed and therefore generally require partial identification. Tian and Pearl first derived theoretically sharp bounds for binary PoCs, including the probability of necessity (PN), the probability of sufficiency (PS), and the probability of necessity and sufficiency (PNS). Mueller et al. subsequently tightened the bounds for binary PNS by incorporating causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. More recently, Li and Pearl, as well as Shu et al., extended PoCs to multivalued settings and derived corresponding theoretical bounds. These developments naturally raise the question of whether additional causal knowledge can further tighten the bounds in multivalued settings. This paper addresses this question by deriving tighter bounds for multivalued PoCs through the incorporation of causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. We illustrate the theoretical results with toy examples, while simulation studies further demonstrate that the proposed bounds are tighter than existing nonbinary bounds.

</details>


### [96] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数(CAS)，一种用于因果解释的紧凑评分架构。CAS从已识别的干预博弈联盟出发，通过因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，并将其转换为局部CAS、带符号的局部CAS及两种全局CAS汇总。其创新不在于新的Shapley公式，而在于具有明确干预目标的局部到全局因果报告层。基准测试和两个实证数据集证明了其优于传统预测性解释方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性解释方法仅归因模型输出，而不归因于对真实世界结果的干预效应。现有方法无法区分"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"的区别，需要一种能明确干预目标的因果解释框架。

Method: 提出CAS架构：(1)从已识别的干预性联盟博弈出发；(2)通过因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比；(3)将原始结果尺度的效应转换为局部CAS、带符号局部CAS和两种互补的全局CAS汇总。关键在于局部到全局的因果报告层，具有明确的干预目标。

Result: 已知真值基准的8次重复主效应交互模拟(n=2,200,三个动作)中，联盟感知CAS的平均局部MAE为0.107，优于one-at-a-time归一化(0.173)和全局归一化绝对ATE向量(0.213)。在401(k)和宾夕法尼亚州再就业奖金两个实证数据集中，预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS的效应修饰因子排名存在显著差异。

Conclusion: CAS将预测结果与解释因果效应异质性分离，提供了独特的价值。在交互作用下优势显著增强，能够识别预测排名中不显著的因果效应修饰因子。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [97] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出trie自动机，一种针对有限集合字符串选择的专用受限解码机制，通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每节点token掩码，绕过通用语法编译的"基数墙"，实现最高29倍的端到端吞吐提升，同时保证100%输出有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要生成符合预定义schema的结构化输出，其中常见约束是从有限有效字符串集合中选择。现有受限解码系统依赖通用语法编译（如XGrammar），当有效值数量增长到数千时会出现严重的性能瓶颈（"基数墙"），编译和逐步骤token计算都变得极慢。

Method: 提出trie自动机，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），采用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每节点的token掩码。预计算掩码使得服务路径变为无状态，可绕过引导解码流水线，从而在批量服务中放大性能优势。

Result: 与XGrammar相比：逐步有效token计算快7倍（0.65us vs 5.8us）；K>=300时编译快2-6.5倍；端到端vLLM吞吐达219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s（批量256，29倍提升）；在7种tokenizer族（32K-262K词表）下，K=10,000时编译保持亚100ms，逐步骤成本与集合大小无关，且100%输出有效。

Conclusion: trie自动机通过挖掘有限集合约束的独特结构，结合预计算掩码策略，既实现了显著的算法加速，又解锁了集成路径层面的优化，是高效的受限解码替代方案，可扩展到大规模有效值集合。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [98] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用多个开源LLM组成的陪审团配合主持人共识机制来替代单一前沿模型法官，用于识别推理链中的缺陷。该系统通过陪审员互相质询、修改投票、主持人达成共识的方式，在识别推理缺陷上显著超越前沿模型（如opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro），且成本仅为前沿模型的8-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理LLM的改进需要高质量评判长推理链的能力，用于数据筛选、强化学习训练信号和性能评估。然而，单一模型（即使是前沿模型）难以准确识别推理缺陷；同时，在在线训练中使用前沿模型常受使用条款限制。因此需要一种既有高保真评判能力、又成本可控且可用开源模型的解决方案。

Method: Reasoning Jury用一个由LLM组成的"陪审团"替代单一法官。流程包括：1) 每位陪审员独立对推理链的缺陷给出初始判断；2) 主持人组织陪审员进行讨论，陪审员之间互相质询和批评彼此的判断；3) 陪审员可修改初始投票；4) 主持人通过讨论达成共识或整合各判断形成最终裁决，包括缺陷类型和严重程度。

Result: 使用开源权重模型（如gpt-oss-120b）作为陪审员的Reasoning Jury在正确识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿模型（opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, gemini-3.1-pro）。同时，陪审团的聚合成本（初始裁决、讨论、整合等）仅为使用前沿模型作为法官的成本的8-15%。此外，这些判断还可用于深入理解推理LLM在基准测试中的失败模式。

Conclusion: Reasoning Jury证明了多模型陪审团+共识机制在推理缺陷识别任务上优于单一前沿模型，且成本大幅降低，为开源、可靠的推理质量评判提供了可行方案。该方法还可助力深入分析模型失败模式，推动推理LLM的改进。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [99] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的代理型AI系统，用于甲状腺超声诊断，整合病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成等任务，并以可审计的病例级证据记录存储输出。系统基于约30万张超声图像和2.4万份配对的OpenThyroidDB数据集开发，在28,458个非重叠测试病例上评估。在结节分割上达到87.21%的Dice分数，良恶性分类AUROC为0.9466，同时支持淋巴结转移预测和滤泡性vs乳头状甲状腺癌分类。证据驱动的报告生成优于多模态大语言模型基线，并显著提升了医生的诊断准确率和一致性，同时减少了报告时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲状腺超声AI系统大多孤立处理单个任务（病灶定位、测量、风险分层、报告生成），缺乏任务间的协调以及对临床审查的有限支持。作者希望构建一个统一的、可交互的代理型AI系统，将多个诊断任务整合到同一工作流中，并提供可审计的证据记录，以支持临床医生的审查和修正。

Method: 构建了一个临床医生可交互的代理型AI系统（ThyroidXAgent），协调多个专用诊断工具，并将其输出存储为可审计的病例级证据记录。同时开发了多中心、多任务数据集OpenThyroidDB（约30万张超声图像和2.4万份配对报告），引入了新的病灶级临床语义指标ThyClinScore用于评估报告质量，并采用证据驱动的报告组装方法替代多模态语言模型。

Result: 在28,458个非重叠测试病例（含来自35个中心的8,721例私人NHC-MISD-TUS队列）中：结节分割平均Dice为87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC为0.9466，淋巴结转移预测AUROC为0.864，滤泡性vs乳头状甲状腺癌分类AUROC为0.805。证据驱动的报告组装优于多模态语言模型基线。ThyClinScore与位置感知语言模型评判器相关性最强。医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可审计、临床医生可纠正的代理型AI系统能够有效支持甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成，在多任务协调、临床实用性以及人机协作方面均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [100] [VALG: An Agentic System for ML Theory Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13060)
*Dechen Zhang,Xuan Tang,Xinxiang Yin,Xingwu Chen,Jian Qian,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VALG，一个将机器学习理论研究的整个流程（问题表述、验证、证明构建）组织为自主智能体工作流的系统。VALG结合多级验证、自适应问题表述和图结构证明开发，在COLT 2026开放问题的九个子问题上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习理论研究通常需要研究者协同开发问题表述、定理目标和证明机制。作者希望探索这一过程能否被组织为自主的智能体工作流，从而系统化地推进ML理论研究。

Method: VALG系统包含三个核心组件：1) 多级验证（Verification）——在源相关定理分支中维护固定的数学规范并检查定理级组合；2) 自适应问题表述（Adaptive formulation）——在表述层面遇到障碍时生成明确相关的变体或放宽；3) 图结构证明开发（Graph-structured proof development）——按依赖顺序构建和审查局部证明，并识别障碍来源（推导、证明结构或定理表述）以路由下一步尝试。

Result: 在五个COLT 2026开放问题的九个子问题上的评估中，两次运行产生了与源问题范围匹配的内部最终定理候选，其余七次产生了受限方法结果、特例或有条件定理。

Conclusion: VALG展示了自主智能体工作流在ML理论研究中的可行性，能够清晰区分源范围匹配、放宽、条件结果和受阻尝试，且系统已开源。

Abstract: Machine learning theory studies learning procedures through mathematical setups in which the data model, training protocol, oracle access, loss, metric, and randomness define the phenomenon that a theorem is meant to explain. Solving an open problem therefore requires the problem formulation, theorem target, and proof mechanism to be developed in concert. Researchers formulate hypotheses, test them through preliminary theoretical or empirical analysis, and refine both assumptions and proofs. We investigate whether this process can be organized as an autonomous agentic workflow for ML theory research.
  We develop VALG, an agentic system that combines multi-level Verification, Adaptive formulation of Learning-theory problems, and Graph-structured proof development. Within each source-relative theorem branch, VALG maintains a fixed mathematical specification, checks the theorem-level composition of a typed proof-dependency graph, and constructs and reviews local proofs in dependency order. When a proof attempt fails, VALG identifies whether the obstruction lies in a derivation, the proof structure, or the theorem formulation and routes the next attempt accordingly. Formulation-level obstructions initiate an explicitly related variant or relaxation, preserving the mathematical relation between the resulting theorem and the source problem.
  We evaluate VALG on nine subproblems from five COLT 2026 open problems. Two runs produce internally finalized theorem candidates that match the scope of their source briefs; the remaining seven yield restricted-method results, special cases, or conditional theorems. These case studies show how VALG keeps source-scope matches, relaxations, conditional results, and blocked attempts mathematically distinct. VALG is open source at https://github.com/DechenZhang/VALG-ML-Theory-Agent.

</details>


### [101] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiG-bench（Discovery in Games），一个包含70个独立游戏的基准测试，用于评估AI智能体在未知目标环境中通过实验和交互发现新规则（即知识发现）的能力。游戏分为7个难度等级，其中21个公开，其余保密用于安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现——即形成新颖的归纳概括——是科学过程的核心，但当前AI基准测试中缺少直接探测AI在受控环境中通过实验发现新知识（且目标未知）的能力的基准，存在明显的评估空白。

Method: DiG-bench包含70个独立的游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串并具有独特的变换规则，需要通过交互和实验来发现。游戏关卡提供一系列挑战以测试规则是否被发现，且各关卡的获胜条件也是未知的。基准分为7个难度等级，其中21个游戏公开发布，其余保密以保障评估安全。

Result: 最低难度等级可被多种模型常规解决，而最高难度等级对最佳的智能体框架模型构成挑战。所有70个游戏均至少有一个人首次尝试即成功解决，证明了基准的人类可解性。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准测试中知识发现能力评估的空白，提供了从简单到极具挑战性的多级难度设计，且通过人类可解性和私密/公开测试集划分确保了评估的有效性和安全性。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [102] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SysName系统，通过模型API解决多轮对话中约束撤销失效（行为复发/撤销惯性）的问题。系统包含三个组件：合同账本（将约束与可执行检查器配对并记录撤销墓碑）、顺序消融探针（预测交付前的逐子句依从性）、修复阶梯（在匹配预算下修复）。实验表明，提前编译能显著减少复发，探针能预测复发，墓碑注释可恢复约三分之一的编译效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户既可以轻松施加约束也可以轻松撤销约束，但模型在约束被撤销后仍会继续执行已撤回的要求，这种"行为复发"现象缺乏测量工具、预测手段和修复方法。现有系统无法逐子句测量约束影响、无法在交付前预测复发、也无法在匹配预算下修复该问题，三个关键缺口亟待填补。

Method: SysName通过模型API实现三个核心组件：(1)合同账本：将每个约束与可执行检查器配对，将撤销记录为墓碑，并将净约束状态预先编译为单一规范；(2)顺序消融探针：测量逐子句依从性和增量行为影响；(3)修复阶梯：在token和尝试匹配预算下操作。整体通过单一规范提前编译净约束状态来解决撤销惯性。

Result: 在HumanEval任务集上（包含多个已验证检查器），8B模型在约束负载增加时复发率从延迟两轮后的值攀升至延迟八轮后的值（具体数值被占位符替换），而更强模型处于最低水平。提前编译相比无账本的验证重试基线显著减少复发（差异有统计显著性，95% CI，p值显著）。探针在交付前预测复发（AUROC较高）；一句墓碑注释恢复约三分之一的编译效果并通过安慰剂对照。递进式修复阶梯未增加可检测的增益（95%置信度排除了较大增益）。

Conclusion: 通过约2倍交付开销和每结果约2倍的API计算成本，撤销失败从对话状态中不可见的属性转变为可测量、可预测、可修复的属性。SysName证明仅通过模型API即可实现这一转变，为多轮对话中的约束管理提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [103] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出@skills开源协议，将skill安装的三大功能（内容、持久化、自动触发）解耦，仅触发需要提示词驻留。通过路径寻址、按需读取、Git跟踪复制等机制，实现"少安装、多使用"的开放skill分发协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前skill分发以安装为主导模式，安装后skill描述驻留在系统提示词中，仅剩不到100个可靠触发槽位，导致长尾skill无法被使用，且团队自有playbook也面临稀缺空间竞争。安装模式将内容、持久化、自动触发三个本可分离的功能捆绑在一起，产生了不必要的资源浪费。

Method: 提出@skills协议：1) 路径(address)可寻址任意skill、子树或集合，读取即使用，无需安装驻留；2) vendor操作将路径上的skill复制到项目Git跟踪树中以便适配和所有权归属；3) link操作仅增加一行.gitignore风格配置（这是唯一消耗提示词驻留的元素）；4) 目录即菜单，bundle变为普通目录；5) 无需manifest、lockfile或注册，SKILL.md保持不变；6) 以单指令文件将任何能读文件、执行命令的agent转变为客户端。

Result: 协议已开源发布，实现在AdaL CLI中，并配套可选的atskills.one中心提供语料库级搜索、排名、无仓库托管、私有/团队集合并支持一屏创作。中心为可选组件，gh:和本地路径无需中心即可解析，索引的GitHub skill保留其gh:身份。

Conclusion: 通过解耦安装的三大功能，@skills实现了"少安装、多使用"的skill分发理念，解决了提示词驻留空间的稀缺问题，为长尾skill提供了可用的路径，同时保持了协议的简洁性和可扩展性。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [104] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wiggle Framework，一个用于测试LLM裁判(judge)认知稳定性(epistemic stability)的统一压力测试框架。框架将裁判的鲁棒性分解为三个维度：机械一致性(重提示/重构下的稳定)、单轮信念(单次挑战下的稳定)和多轮持久性(持续/自适应压力下的稳定)。研究对9个前沿模型、14个判断任务进行测试，发现所有模型都存在显著"摇摆"——静态推拉下翻转判决25-71%，对抗性说服下翻转62-91%。关键发现是，成功的压力改变几乎总是损害与真实标签的一致性。裁判陪审团多数基线强度是预测哪些条目会摇摆的最有效单次信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判已成为模型评估、在线评分和奖励建模的核心基础设施，但现有验证仅基于黄金数据上的准确率，无法说明裁判在重新提示、挑战或持续施压下是否稳定。这是一个关键盲区，因为裁判的不稳定性可能导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 提出Wiggle Framework，将裁判鲁棒性分解为三个维度：机械一致性(重提示和重构下稳定)、单轮信念(单次挑战下稳定)、多轮持久性(持续或自适应压力下稳定)。对9个前沿模型在14个判断任务(涵盖安全、毒性、AI写作检测、政治回应评估)上进行系统性压力测试。

Result: 所有模型都表现出显著的裁判"摇摆"：静态推拉下25-71%翻转判决，对抗性LLM说服者下62-91%翻转。成功改变裁判判决的压力几乎总是对真实标签造成净损害。陪审团多数基线强度是最有效的单次预测信号。

Conclusion: 这是首次在判断语境下对机械一致性、从众性和可说服性测试进行跨数据集的同类比较。论文揭示了LLM裁判存在普遍的认知不稳定性，且压力造成的改变倾向于偏离真实答案而非靠近，同时提出了陪审团多数强度作为预测裁判摇摆的有效信号。

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth. Beyond the framework itself, we identify baseline jury majority strength as the most effective single-shot signal for anticipating which items wiggle. Taken together, this is the first apples-to-apples cross-dataset comparison of mechanical, conformity, and persuadability tests in a judging context.

</details>


### [105] [SteerBench-Work: A Benchmark for Agent Steering at Action Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12654)
*Oguz Serdar,Cuneyt Mertayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SteerBench-Work，一个以真实事件为锚点、双向的工作场所智能体门控决策基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在执行工具操作前是否应"继续"或"暂停"的决策能力。基准包含106个场景、证据反转镜像和校准控制，并发现模型的失败几乎完全偏向于过度持有（误拦27.1%）而非误放（1.0%），且能力更高的模型往往在提交边界过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行LLM智能体通过工具执行关键操作（如发送邮件、合并PR、转账），单步操作的"提交前门控决策"（继续还是暂停）至关重要，但缺乏专门评估该决策能力的系统化基准。

Method: 构建SteerBench-Work基准：(1)以公开安全事件为锚点创建106个场景；(2)设计证据反转的镜像对以测试鲁棒性；(3)加入校准控制；(4)proceed与hold标签近乎均匀分布，确保两个错误方向获得相近的测试机会；模型需根据提议动作和可用证据输出门控决策。

Result: 在30个模型条件下：模型错误持有已授权且证据充分的工作占28.1%，而错误允许不安全工作仅占1.0%。风险已解决提交是最难场景；模型在证据反转镜像上的表现(63.8%)远差于原始事件(98.5%)。更高能力模型往往过度拒绝，增加推理可修复弱门控但对校准良好的门控无提升。

Conclusion: 通用能力不等于门控校准能力，工作场所智能体的安全部署需要专门的提交边界决策校准，单纯提升模型能力或推理深度无法解决过度拒绝问题，需要针对性基准（如SteerBench-Work）来度量和改进门控决策。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents act through tools, and a single step can send an email, merge a pull request, or wire a payment. The steering decision is the pre-commit choice at that boundary: proceed, or hold for human or policy review. We introduce SteerBench-Work, an incident-anchored, bidirectional benchmark for that decision in workplace agents across developer operations, customer service, finance, legal, medical, HR, and security.
  Release v2026-05 contains 106 scenarios anchored in public incidents, paired evidence-reversed mirrors, and calibration controls, with labels split nearly evenly between proceed and hold so the two error directions get near-identical numbers of chances. A model sees the proposed action and the available evidence, returns a gate decision, and is scored on whether it crosses or holds the boundary correctly. Across 30 model conditions the failures run almost entirely in one direction: models wrongly hold authorized, evidence-cleared work on 28.1% of opportunities and wrongly allow unsafe work on 1.0%. The hardest cases are risk-resolved commits, where signed or structured evidence has already cleared a real risk trigger, and models score markedly worse on evidence-reversed mirrors of famous incidents (63.8%) than on the incidents themselves (98.5%). General capability is not the same as steering calibration: higher-capability models often over-refuse at the commit boundary, and more reasoning can repair a weak gate while leaving a calibrated one flat. The public leaderboard is at steerbench.com.

</details>


### [106] [Multi-Layer Context Camouflaging: A Semantic Superposition and Contextual Lamination Framework for Malpractice-Resilient Online Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13100)
*Gupta Lovi Raj,Kaur Kamalpreet,Dama Sri Ram,Parani Prajithaa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多層上下文偽裝理論（MCCT），作為對現有在線評估系統的擴展，通過語義疊加技術保護渲染的評估內容免受截圖、屏幕共享、OCR和自動化抓取等提取攻擊，同時確保合法考生可正常讀取內容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 現有在線評估系統（瀏覽器鎖定、攝像頭監控、行為分析）雖能防止作弊，但無法防禦針對評估內容本身的提取攻擊（截圖、屏幕共享、OCR、自動化抓取）。需要一種數學框架來保護渲染內容的機密性。

Method: 在MARS框架下的MSCCM模型中引入MCCT數學框架，核心思路是將真實評估內容與合成偽裝內容在語義層面疊加為統一渲染，僅合法候選者可恢復。框架包括六個耦合構造：上下文反演算子、上下文層壓算子、分離通道、人類可讀性泛函、計算模糊性泛函和上下文偽裝張量。計算模糊性用條件熵量化未授權提取的不確定性，合法恢復通過精確濾波恆等式保證。

Result: 建立了多項理論性質（模糊性、偽裝密度、語義保持、多觀測洩漏、時間多路複用），並提出具有計算複雜度分析的渲染算法及預註冊評估協議。

Conclusion: MCCT為行為適應性、可訪問性感知、計算韌性的數字評估提供了數學嚴謹的基礎，在保護渲染評估內容的同時保持合法用戶的可讀性。

Abstract: Contemporary online assessment systems rely primarily on browser lockdown, webcam monitoring, and behavioural analytics, yet remain vulnerable to attacks that extract the assessment content itself through screenshots, screen sharing, optical character recognition, and automated scraping. This paper extends the Multi-dimensional Spatio-Temporal Context Camouflaging Model (MSCCM) within the MARS (Multi-modal Assessment Resilience Suite) by introducing the Multi-Layer Context Camouflaging Theory (MCCT), a mathematical framework that protects rendered assessment content through semantic superposition. Authentic assessment content and synthetically generated camouflage are represented as a unified rendering while remaining recoverable only by legitimate candidates. The framework models the adversarial extraction process through an explicit extraction-channel operator and develops six coupled constructs: the Context Inversion Operator, Contextual Lamination Operator, Separation Channel, Human Readability Functional, Computational Ambiguity Functional, and Context Camouflage Tensor. Computational ambiguity is formulated using conditional entropy, yielding a closed-form expression that quantifies uncertainty during unauthorized extraction, while legitimate recovery is guaranteed through an exact filtering identity. We further establish theoretical properties governing ambiguity, camouflage density, semantic preservation, multi-observation leakage, and temporal multiplexing, and present a rendering algorithm with computational complexity and a pre-registered evaluation protocol. MCCT provides a mathematically rigorous foundation for behaviorally adaptive, accessibility-aware, and computationally resilient digital assessment by securing rendered assessment content while preserving readability for legitimate users.

</details>


### [107] [Designing AI Pipelines for Decision-Ready ITSM Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12670)
*Archan Dutta,Yash Dharmadhikari,Marat Valiullin,Rahul Guha,Alexander Liss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个社会技术AI流水线，将原始IT服务管理（ITSM）工单数据转化为多层次的决策支持工具，采用LLM模式规范化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类和层次凝聚聚类，生成面向高管的Main-topics和细粒度Sub-topics。通过六项工件和五位来自销售工程与客户成功角色的评估者验证，四项决策支持指标（可解释性、可操作性、信任度、使用可能性）平均分均超过4.0/5.0，其中信任度是最一致的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ITSM系统积累了海量异构工单数据，但销售和高管利益相关者难以将这些数据转化为可操作的情报，存在数据-决策之间的转化鸿沟。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，构建了结合LLM模式规范化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类及层次凝聚聚类的AI流水线，生成多层次决策支持工件。

Result: 六项工件经五名销售工程和客户成功角色评估者评价，四项决策支持指标（可解释性、可操作性、信任度、使用可能性）平均均超过4.0/5.0，信任度是最稳定的积极信号。

Conclusion: ITSM分析应被视为信息系统（IS）中的转化、抽象和人本设计问题，本研究为将原始工单数据转化为高层决策支持提供了可行的社会技术路径。

Abstract: IT service management (ITSM) systems accumulate large volumes of heterogeneous ticket data that are difficult for sales and executive stakeholders to convert into actionable intelligence. This paper presents a sociotechnical AI pipeline, designed and evaluated following design science research principles, that transforms raw ITSM exports into a multilevel decision-support artifact. The pipeline combines LLM-based schema normalization, HDBSCAN sub-topic clustering, and hierarchical agglomerative clustering to generate executive-facing Main-topics and granular Sub-topics. A stakeholder evaluation across six artifacts and five raters from Sales Engineering and customer success roles shows that all four decision-support metrics, interpretability, actionability, trust, and likelihood of use, on average exceed 4.0 out of 5.0, with trust as the most consistent signal. The findings position ITSM analytics as an Information Systems (IS) problem of transformation, abstraction, and human-centered design.

</details>


### [108] [On the Expressive Power of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12671)
*Phokion Kolaitis,Rik Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用电路复杂性理论来刻画Transformer表达能力的相关工作，重点介绍如何通过注意力机制和精度等资源参数化Transformer，并与不同类别的电路复杂性类进行直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer是当今所有大语言模型的核心组件，但对其作为语言识别器的表达能力缺乏精确刻画。现有工作越来越多地使用电路复杂性理论来校准Transformer的表达能力，因为Transformer的资源（如注意力和精度）与电路资源（如门类型、大小和深度）存在天然对应关系，需要一个系统的综述来梳理这些成果。

Method: 采用电路复杂性理论作为分析框架，将Transformer的各种资源参数（注意力机制、精度等）映射到电路复杂性类（如门类型、电路大小和深度等资源），从而建立Transformer与经典计算模型之间的直接比较，并系统综述已有研究成果。

Result: 提供了一系列利用电路复杂性概念和方法刻画Transformer表达能力的重要研究成果综述，阐明了Transformer在不同资源约束下对应的电路复杂性类别。

Conclusion: 电路复杂性已被证明是分析Transformer表达能力的正确理论框架，为精确理解大语言模型的计算能力提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-layer transformers form the critical component of essentially all large language models (LLMs) in use today. Because of their ubiquity and computational capability, there is a rapidly growing body of work that aims to precisely calibrate the expressive power of transformers as language recognizers by comparing them against standard models of computation studied for decades by the theoretical computer science community. In this endeavor, circuit complexity has by and large emerged as the "correct" branch of computational complexity to analyze the expressive power of transformers; the reason is that parameterizing transformers by the various resources they use, such as attention and precision, leads to direct comparisons with different classes of circuits parameterized by resources such as type of gates, size, and depth. Here, we present an overview of selected results that delineate the expressive power of transformers using concepts and methods from circuit complexity.

</details>
