To address this challenge, we augmented a digital puzzle-based learning game for decision tree construction with an adaptive feedback module generating individualized messages based on the continuous evaluation of learners' problem-solving strategies. Building on an earlier baseline study, the present work investigates how this strategy-oriented feedback shapes students' problem-solving processes. For this purpose, screencast video data and gameplay logs (N=205, approx. 55 hours of gameplay footage) are used to enable fine-grained insights into learners' strategic behavior, its persistence, and transitions. The findings demonstrate how strategy-oriented feedback can support the development of structured problem-solving skills in decision tree construction and inform the design of ML learning environments that foster transferable competencies in secondary computing education.

</details>


### [175] [Proactive Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12649)
*Joonhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是关于主动计算（proactive computing）的综述，定义了计算系统从被动响应工具向主动感知、预测并采取行动的范式转变，并将主动计算刻画为一个跨感知、理解、决策、行动和治理的系统级集成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算系统正从反应式工具转向在用户明确请求之前就感知、解释、预测并行动的系统。这种转变由移动连接、可穿戴与环境感知、机器学习与基础模型、分布式边缘计算及物理驱动的全球扩展所推动，因此需要对这一新兴范式进行系统性的定义与梳理。

Method: 本文通过综述方法：(1) 给出主动计算的形式化定义；(2) 将其与反应式、上下文感知、自适应和预测式计算区分开来；(3) 将主动性框架化为一个系统级集成问题；(4) 回顾技术使能因素；(5) 组织设计空间；(6) 分析不确定性触发的技术挑战与预测到行动的鸿沟；(7) 讨论用户接受度、信任、隐私、问责、公平性和可持续性等社会技术问题。

Result: 文章识别出主动计算的核心研究挑战并非仅仅是提升预测准确性，而是确定系统应在何时、如何以及是否代表用户采取行动。

Conclusion: 主动计算的关键在于系统级集成与治理框架的构建，研究重心应从预测精度转向行动决策的时机与合理性。

Abstract: Computing systems are moving from reactive tools toward systems that sense, interpret, predict, and act before explicit user requests. This transition is enabled by the global scale of mobile connectivity, the rapid expansion of wearable and ambient sensing, advances in machine learning and foundation models, distributed edge infrastructure, and physical actuation. We define \emph{proactive computing} as a paradigm in which systems infer user context, anticipate future needs or risks, and initiate information delivery or actions at an appropriate time. This survey distinguishes proactive computing from reactive, context-aware, adaptive, and predictive computing, and frames proactivity as a system-level integration problem across sensing, understanding, decision making, action, and governance. We review the technological enablers of proactive computing, organize its design space, analyze technical challenges such as uncertainty-aware triggering and the prediction-to-action gap, and discuss socio-technical issues involving user acceptance, trust, privacy, accountability, fairness, and sustainability. We argue that the key research challenge is not merely improving prediction accuracy, but determining when, how, and whether systems should act on behalf of users.

</details>


### [176] [From Fair Representation to Just Recognition in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12669)
*Severin Engelmann,Daniel Susser*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式AI正在改变公平AI/ML文献中分配公平与表征公平之间的平衡。作者认为，对于生成式AI而言，表征伤害已成为价值对齐的核心，但现有的"表征准确性"策略存在根本局限。作者借鉴Nancy Fraser的政治理论（参与平等），主张应从"表征公平"转向"承认正义"，为生成式AI的公平治理提供更好的概念与规范性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平AI文献将分配公平与表征公平区分开来，但生成式AI（尤其是LLM）本质上是表达性系统，其主要功能是传达意义而非自动化决策，因此表征危害变得核心。然而，现有的表征公平方法依赖"描述准确性"，这存在三个关键局限：(1)许多社会群体没有稳定或界限清晰的指涉对象可被衡量准确性；(2)谁有权决定何为错误表征不明确；(3)即使准确表征也可能再生产有害的社会模式。作者认为根本问题不是表征错误，而是"错误承认"（misrecognition）。

Method: 本文采用规范性政治理论分析方法，借鉴Nancy Fraser的"参与平等"（participatory parity）框架，论证从"表征公平"（representational fairness）转向"承认正义"（recognitional justice）的必要性，并将其应用于生成式AI的公平治理挑战。

Result: 作者论证了"表征准确性"作为生成式AI公平标准的不足，并提出"承认正义"框架能更好地处理生成式AI中社会群体表征的核心公平挑战，为治理提供了更充分的概念与规范性工具。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的公平问题不应被简化为表征是否准确，而应被理解为承认正义问题——即关于社会群体在AI表达中是否获得平等的参与地位和被承认的地位。Fraser的参与平等框架为这一转向提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The fair AI/ML literature has long distinguished distributive fairness, concerning how automated systems allocate resources and opportunities, from representational fairness, concerning how they shape the ways individuals and social groups are perceived, understood, and accorded social status. Generative AI is rebalancing these normative dimensions. Unlike predictive systems, large language models (LLMs) and related technologies are fundamentally expressive: their primary function is to convey meaning rather than automate domain-specific decisions. Representational harm has also become central to value alignment, especially in research on what and whose values and perspectives AI systems should represent. Existing approaches to harms in the representation of social groups often appeal to descriptive accuracy, but this strategy has important limitations. For many social groups, no stable or bounded referent exists against which representational accuracy can be judged. It is also unclear who has the authority to decide what counts as misrepresentation, while even accurate representations can reproduce harmful social patterns. The underlying problem, we argue, is therefore not simply misrepresentation but misrecognition. Drawing on political theory, especially Nancy Fraser's account of participatory parity, we show how moving from representational fairness to recognitional justice provides better conceptual and normative tools for governing central fairness challenges in generative AI.

</details>


### [177] [A Generative Framework for the Creation of Multi-Attribute Geographically-Explicit Synthetic Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12768)
*Jinlin Wu,Si Qiao,Yi Liu,Fuzhen Yin,Na Jiang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于层次扩散模型的生成式框架，能够从聚合数据中重建区域特定的多属性联合分布，并生成带有明确家庭和工作位置的地理显式合成人口。在50个美国州及华盛顿特区上生成了3.32亿个体的全国合成人口，在联合分布重建上优于IPF和单步扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以仅从聚合数据重建区域特定的联合分布，而多属性合成人口需要真实的联合分布和地理变异，这对微观模拟和基于智能体的建模至关重要。

Method: 提出一个层次扩散的生成框架，以区域特定的多属性联合分布为训练目标，生成合成个体并同时为其分配显式的家庭和工作位置。

Result: 在50个州及华盛顿特区上生成包含332,387,543个个体的全国地理显式合成人口，涵盖五个属性（年龄、性别、就业、教育、收入）。留出法区域实验表明，对比迭代比例拟合（IPF）和单次扩散基线，该框架在联合分布重建上表现更优，且位置分配保留了主要的居住和工作模式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展的生成式方法，可在区域和国家层面创建地理显式的合成人口，通过重建区域特定联合分布，能为地理模拟（如基于智能体建模）引入更真实的行为，从而探索复杂城市现象的涌现。

Abstract: Generating multi-attribute synthetic populations with realistic joint distributions and geographic variation is a foundational requirement for geo-simulation techniques, such as micro-simulation and agent-based modeling. However, it remains challenging for existing methods to reconstruct region-specific joint distributions from aggregated-level data alone. Thus, we propose a hierarchical diffusion-based generative framework that utilizes a realistic region-specific joint distribution of multiple attributes as the training target to create a synthetic population along with assigning their explicit home and work locations. Applied to 50 U.S. states and Washington, D.C., this framework generates a nationwide geographically-explicit synthetic population consisting of 332,387,543 individuals with five attributes (e.g., age, gender, employment, education, income). Held-out regional experiments show improved reconstruction of joint distributions relative to Iterative Proportional Fitting (IPF) and a one-shot diffusion baseline. At the same time, the location assignment preserves major residential and workplace patterns. As such, the proposed framework provides a scalable generative approach for creating geographically explicit synthetic populations at both regional and national levels. By reconstructing region-specific joint distributions of these five attributes using this framework, the resulting synthetic population could introduce more realistic behaviors into geo-simulations, such as agent-based modeling, enabling further exploration of the emergence of complex urban phenomena through human interactions.

</details>


### [178] [Impact of introducing "Informatics I" to the common university entrance examination in Japan: a longitudinal study on students' perceptions of their information-related knowledge and skills from 2006 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12924)
*Akimasa Morihata*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析2006至2026年东京大学一年级学生的问卷数据，考察日本2025年大学入学统一考试中引入"情报I"科目对学生高中信息学学习的影响。研究发现2013年课程改革未产生明显变化，而2025年新入学考试的引入则导致学生在计算机科学主题掌握报告上出现显著变化，且这一变化难以用实际能力提升解释，更适合理解为学生自我判断标准的转变（washback效应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息学教育涵盖从计算机科学知识到ICT技能的广泛内容，而大规模高风险的标准化评估可能扭曲教育。尽管近年来信息学课程与评估发展迅速，但评估对信息学教育的影响尚未得到充分研究。本研究旨在通过日本大学入学统一考试引入"情报I"这一案例，探究高风险评估对信息学教育的反向影响（washback效应）。

Method: 使用东京大学2006至2026年每年对所有一年级学生进行的信息相关知识与技能自我感知问卷作为数据来源，进行纵向研究。通过两组对比来隔离入学考试的影响：（1）对比2013年课程改革与2022年课程改革；（2）对比2025年入学的直接入学学生与间隔年学生（两者修读不同课程但参加同一新考试）。采用washback效应理论框架进行解读。

Result: （1）2013年课程改革没有导致学生感知的明显不连续性；（2）2025年新入学考试的引入在计算机科学主题的掌握报告比例上产生了急剧变化；（3）这一变化幅度过大，难以归结为实际能力提升，更适合理解为学生判断"已掌握"的标准发生了转变。

Conclusion: 大规模高风险的入学考试会对信息学教育产生显著的washback效应，且这种效应主要通过改变学生自我评估的标准来实现，而非实际提升能力。这提示在信息学教育中引入高风险评估时需要谨慎，防止评估扭曲教育内容与目标。

Abstract: Despite the recent intensive development of secondary education curricula and assessments in informatics, the impact of assessments has not been well studied in this field. Since informatics education covers a diverse range of content, from computer science knowledge to ICT skills, careful consideration is needed to prevent assessments from distorting education. This study investigates the impact of introducing ``Informatics I'' into the Common Test for University Admissions in Japan, as an example of a large-scale, standardized, high-stakes assessment in 2025. As the data source for this analysis, this study uses a questionnaire that has been administered every year from 2006 to 2026 to all first-year students at the University of Tokyo. The questionnaire asks students for their self-perceptions of the information-related knowledge and skills they studied and acquired in high school. Using these data, we conduct a longitudinal study of the 2013 curriculum reform, the 2022 reform, and the introduction of the new entrance examination. We attempt to isolate the impact of the entrance examination through two comparisons: between the 2013 curriculum reform and the 2022 reform; and between direct-entry and gap-year students among those entering in 2025, who followed different curricula but took the new examination. We use the theoretical framework of the washback effect as a lens for interpreting these differences. We found that (1) the 2013 curriculum reform produced no discontinuity in students' perceptions, whereas (2) the introduction of the new entrance examination in 2025 produced a sharp change, particularly in the proportion of students reporting acquisition of computer-science topics; and (3) this change is too large to be interpreted as a gain in proficiency, and is better understood as a shift in students' criteria for judging acquisition.

</details>


### [179] [Applied and Filtered: An End-to-End Algorithmic Fairness Audit of A Public Employment Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13022)
*Gemma Galdón-Clavell*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次对巴塞罗那公共就业机构Barcelona Activa使用的半自动化招聘系统TalentClue进行了独立的端到端公平性审计，分析了约49.7万条候选人-职位流水线数据（2017-2022年），发现尽管二元性别层面的总体结果在统计上无显著差异，但按薪资水平、年龄和性别认同细分后存在实质性差异。审计还揭示了供应商与部署方之间的信息不对称问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的算法公平性评估通常将AI系统视为有界的技术组件，忽略了其运行的组织背景。作者认为这种模型层面的评估不足以理解部署系统中实际出现的公平性问题，因此需要对运行中的系统进行端到端、社会技术层面的全面审计。

Method: 对Barcelona Activa运营的TalentClue平台进行独立端到端公平性审计，分析2017年9月至2022年9月间约49.7万条候选人-职位流水线数据，覆盖七个流水线阶段（包括自动化处理、人工裁量、候选人数据和雇主决策），并通过差异影响比率（DIR）等统计指标评估各组别的公平性表现。

Result: 二元性别总体结果在统计上不可区分，但掩盖了实质性差异：女性在中等薪资短名单中出现不利影响（DIR=0.786），20个行业中有15个存在薪资差异，46-55岁女性面临复合劣势（DIR=0.77）；非二元性别候选人的短名单率不足男性的三分之一（DIR=0.295）；55岁及以上候选人在流水线中完全缺失（占巴塞罗那劳动力的15.6%）；性别差距随时间缩小（从2017年的6.5个百分点降至2022年的1.3个百分点）。

Conclusion: 公平性结果源于自动化处理、人工裁量、数据质量、供应商不透明性和流水线结构之间的相互作用。作者基于先前对社会技术、端到端公平性评估的呼吁，实证表明仅靠模型层面的评估不足以理解部署系统中的公平性问题。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness evaluation commonly assesses AI systems as bounded technical components, abstracting away the organizational context in which they operate. We present, to our knowledge, the first independent end-to-end fairness audit of a semi-automated hiring system operated by Barcelona Activa, a public employment agency using the third-party TalentClue platform for candidate search and shortlisting. We analyze approximately 497,000 candidate-vacancy pipeline entries from September 2017 to September 2022, covering seven pipeline stages that span automated processing, human discretion, candidate data, and employer decisions.
  Aggregate outcomes across binary genders are statistically indistinguishable, yet this parity masks substantial disparities by salary level, age, and gender identity. Women face adverse impact in mid-salary shortlisting (DIR = 0.786, p < 0.001), alongside salary disparities in 15 of 20 sectors and a compounded disadvantage for women aged 46-55 (DIR = 0.77). Non-binary candidates are shortlisted at less than one third the rate of men (DIR = 0.295), although this estimate rests on a small sample (N = 285). Candidates aged 55 and over are entirely absent from the pipeline despite comprising 15.6% of Barcelona's labor force. The gender gap in shortlisting narrows over time, from 6.5 percentage points in 2017 to 1.3 in 2022.
  The audit further reveals a vendor-deployer information asymmetry: Barcelona Activa lacks access to key information about TalentClue's matching logic and evaluation. Fairness outcomes can thus arise from interactions among automated processing, human discretion, data quality, vendor opacity, and pipeline structure. We build on prior calls for sociotechnical, end-to-end fairness evaluation, showing empirically why model-level assessment alone can be insufficient for understanding fairness in deployed systems.

</details>


### [180] [Follow the Norm: Accounting for Fine-Tuning and Prompt Effects on Model Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13250)
*Long Hoang Nguyen,Brice Valentin Kok-Shun,Guangyu Du,Ali Sunyaev*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI系统视为"代理行为者"，研究数据集层面的规范如何通过微调使模型偏离其基线安全行为。作者在三个模型（LLaMA-3.2-11B、Qwen-3.5-9B、Pixtral-12B）上使用LoRA微调和提示词操控进行实验，发现破坏规范的微调会使模型从安全遵从转向工具性自利，但系统提示可覆盖此行为。研究支持对齐的分布式视角，即行为取决于训练数据、微调和提示词的共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范数据集常被用于训练和校准AI系统，但其中包含的规范可能作为行动引导模式而非中立道德知识发挥作用。作者希望检验数据集层面的规范是否会改变AI在高冲突困境下的安全基线行为。

Method: 将AI系统视为代理行为者，在三个模型上使用LoRA微调Social Chemistry 101中的公平/作弊（遵循规范vs破坏规范）数据，并结合提示词引导（system prompts）进行受控实验。

Result: 在三个模型中，破坏规范的微调均使模型的默认推理风格从安全遵从转向工具性自利，而系统提示词能够抑制或激发这些模式。实验建立了从下游推理追溯到上游规范的混合方法审计线索。

Conclusion: 结果支持对齐的分布式观点：AI观察到的行为取决于训练数据、微调和提示词的联合作用。作者建议采用规范感知的文档记录和推理日志机制，以实现可争议的监督（contestable oversight）。

Abstract: Normative datasets are often used to train and align AI systems, but the norms they contain can function as action-guiding patterns rather than neutral moral knowledge. We propose treating the AI system as a proxy actor and test whether dataset-level norms can shift it away from its baseline safety behavior when it faces high-conflict dilemmas. We make three contributions. First, we demonstrate in controlled experiments that norm-breaking fine-tuning yields norm-divergent actions justified by self-interested rationales, suggesting a systematic shift in patterns of justification. Second, we establish a practical audit trail linking downstream justifications to upstream norms using mixed methods. Third, we show that system prompts can both suppress and elicit these patterns. We conducted experiments on three models (LLaMA-3.2-11B, Qwen-3.5-9B, and Pixtral-12B) using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning on Social Chemistry 101 Fairness/Cheating (norm-following vs. norm-breaking) with prompt steering. Across all three models, we find that norm-breaking fine-tuning shifts the model's default rationale style from safety compliance to instrumental self-interest, whereas system prompts can override this behavior. Our results support a distributed view of alignment in which observed behavior depends jointly on training data, fine-tuning, and prompting, motivating norm-aware documentation and rationale logging for contestable oversight.

</details>


### [181] [The Use of Learning Management Systems for Self-paced Learning: The Case at a South African Public Access Centre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13351)
*Guidance Mthwazi,Meke Kapepo,Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究以南非公共接入中心（PAC）的I-CAN中心为案例，通过访谈38名学习者并采用主题分析法，探讨了学习管理系统（LMS）在支持自定步调学习中的作用。研究发现PAC在促进ICT技能和数字包容方面发挥关键作用，但网络连接差、基础设施落后和课程指令不清等挑战限制了LMS的有效性，需进行基础设施升级和以用户为中心的设计优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在探索LMS在公共接入中心环境中支持自定步调学习的可行性与局限性，填补社区学习环境中LMS采用研究的空白，并为数字包容和终身学习提供实践见解。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，以南非I-CAN公共接入中心为研究对象，通过对38名学习者进行半结构化访谈收集定性数据，并运用主题分析法对访谈数据进行编码和主题提炼。

Result: 研究发现PAC在推广ICT技能和数字包容方面具有关键作用，能提供灵活的学习资源访问并赋能学习者。LMS的使用增强了便利性，混合学习是学习者的首选方式。然而，网络连接不稳定、基础设施老旧和课程说明不清晰等问题限制了LMS的实施效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在社区学习环境中优化LMS实施需要加强基础设施建设并采用以用户为中心的设计理念，以更好地发挥公共接入中心在数字学习和教育公平方面的潜力。

Abstract: This study investigates the use of a Learning Management System (LMS) to support self-paced learning at a South African Public Access Centre (PAC), using the I-CAN Centre as a case study. Through semi-structured interviews with thirty-eight learners and thematic analysis, the research explores opportunities and challenges associated with LMS adoption. Findings reveal that PACs play a critical role in promoting ICT skills and digital inclusion, offering learners flexible access to learning resources and fostering empowerment. While LMS use enhances convenience and supports blended learning as the preferred approach, persistent challenges, such as poor connectivity, outdated infrastructure, and unclear course instructions, limit its effectiveness. These findings highlight the need for infrastructural upgrades and user-centric design to optimise LMS implementation in community-based learning environments.

</details>


### [182] [Credible, Not Always Correct: How Reddit Users Verify AI-Generated Legal Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13369)
*Rebecca Owens,Yusuf Mücahit Çetinkaya,Stergios Aidinlis,Dhyey Mehta,Tuğrulcan Elmas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对153个Reddit叙事和5,341个社区反应的混合方法分析，揭示了AI生成法律建议的实际效力取决于其社会可信度的生产，而非其准确性。研究发现少数用户通过多模型交叉验证或提交社区评估（"分布式顾问"）来验证AI建议，但绝大多数用户仅凭其律师般的形式和情感安抚就付诸行动，表明AI辅助法律自助运行在一个将验证负担转移给最无能力承担者的非正式基础设施中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被普通人用于解决实际法律问题，尽管现有研究已评估法律AI的准确性，但对机器生成的指导如何被验证并建立足够可信度以促使非专业用户行动的过程知之甚少。

Method: 采用双重方法分析：对153个Reddit叙事进行质性与量化分析，并结合5,341个社区反应，绘制验证实践的谱系。

Result: 发现验证实践呈现光谱分布：少数用户通过跨模型三角验证AI建议，部分用户将AI生成指导提交给平台社区评估（"分布式顾问"配置）；但更普遍的情况是，叙事对验证过程保持沉默，AI法律建议仅凭其律师式形式和情感安慰就被付诸行动。

Conclusion: 研究显示AI辅助法律自助运行于一种新兴的非正式基础设施中，该系统将验证工作重新分配给最不具备承担能力的人群，对法律正义获取构成深层挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by laypeople to resolve real legal problems, against a backdrop of persistent access-to-justice deficits. This article presents evidence that the practical force of AI-generated legal advice depends not on its accuracy but on the social production of its credibility. While existing research has assessed the accuracy of legal AI, less is known about how machine-generated guidance is verified and made credible enough for lay users to act on. Drawing on a dual-method analysis of 153 Reddit narratives and 5,341 community reactions, this article maps a spectrum of verification practices. At one end, a minority of users verify AI-generated legal advice by triangulating across models, and some submit AI-generated guidance to platform communities for evaluation before acting, a configuration we term distributed counsel. Far more commonly, however, narratives are silent on verification. AI-generated legal advice is acted on the strength of its lawyer-like form and emotional reassurance alone. These findings show that AI-assisted legal self-help operates within an emerging informal infrastructure which redistributes the work of verification to those least equipped to bear it.

</details>


### [183] [Algorithmic Gender Prediction Is Illegitimate, But Gender Imputation Can Yield Valid Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13444)
*Evan Dong,Angelina Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文区分了性别预测在伦理上"不合法"(illegitimate, 造成伤害)与在测量上"无效"(invalid, 产生不可用数据)两种失败方式，指出用于公平目的的性别推测(imputation)可能是"不合法但仍有效的"。借助跨性别女性主义文献区分针对女性/女性气质的性别歧视与针对跨性别/非二元人群的性别歧视，主张仅在无法通过其他合理手段实现反歧视效益且伤害最小化时使用性别推测，并通过三个案例研究论证了这一张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习伦理研究者和批判性HCI学者认为算法预测性别是错误的，但同时其他研究者依赖预测性别标签来研究性别差异和开发公平性技术。这两种看似矛盾的观点需要调和，作者希望通过区分"不合法"和"无效"来澄清这一困境。

Method: 作者从合法性和有效性两个维度重新框架关于性别预测的辩论，并引入跨性别女性主义理论区分两种性别歧视形式，然后在三个案例研究（生成图像模型中的性别偏见审计、电影中的性别差异测量、从人名推测性别）中检验这一张力框架。

Result: 性别推测可以产生有效的差异测量，但同时可能是不合法且对跨性别和非二元人群有害的；现行辩论混淆了合法性与有效性这两个不同关注点，既掩盖了性别推测可以支持公平努力的场景，也掩盖了其对跨性别/非二元人群无法捕捉的伤害。

Conclusion: 应仅在无法通过其他合理手段实现反歧视效益且伤害最小化的条件下使用性别推测，并建议开发更包容的方法以应对所有形式的性别歧视。

Abstract: Machine learning ethics researchers and critical HCI scholars have argued that algorithmically predicting gender is wrong. At the same time, other researchers rely on predicted gender labels to study gender disparities and develop algorithmic fairness techniques. How do we reconcile these two seemingly contradictory intuitions? We differentiate two ways gender prediction may be wrong: being illegitimate, thereby contributing to harm; and being invalid, thereby producing unusable measurements. Our analysis translates arguments against gender prediction into these terms of legitimacy and validity and shows how gender imputation applied for fairness purposes can be illegitimate yet still yield valid disparity measurements. We clarify this bind by drawing upon transfeminist literature to distinguish sexism that targets women and femininity from sexism that targets transgender and nonbinary people. While gender imputation can produce valid measurements for the former, it is illegitimate and harmful for the latter. We argue that practitioners should deploy gender imputation only when it would achieve anti-discrimination benefits that cannot be achieved through other reasonable means, while harms are minimized to the extent possible. We examine this tension in three case studies: auditing gender bias in generative image models, measuring gender disparities in film, and imputing gender from personal names. By disentangling legitimacy from validity, and differentiating these two forms of sexism, we show how debates over gender prediction have conflated distinct concerns, obscuring both the settings in which gender imputation can support fairness efforts and the harms towards transgender and nonbinary people that it fundamentally cannot capture. We conclude by recommending the development of more inclusive methods that address all kinds of sexism.

</details>

### [184] [What Makes a Peer? Valuation-Anchored Similarity in Private Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12594)
*Sebastian Frank,Jingrao Lyu,Max Jarmey,Preetha Saha,Mingshu Li,Sweet Kaur,Sola Akinola,Dhagash Mehta*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 提出一个基于集成树的监督相似性学习框架（使用CatBoost），通过市场估值视角定义公司相似性，而非静态特征匹配或语义描述。从重要性加权的叶节点共现中推导估值感知的相似性度量，在约27万家全球私募公司数据上验证优于传统距离和文本嵌入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私募市场中透明度有限、披露稀少、交易不频繁，寻找经济意义上相似的同行公司用于估值、尽调、组合构建和风险管理是根本性挑战。传统基于静态特征匹配或语义描述的方法无法捕捉驱动估值的真实相似性。

Method: 在观测到的私募公司估值上训练CatBoost梯度提升决策树模型，从集成中重要性加权的叶节点共现推导估值感知的相似性度量。该度量能捕捉共享的估值驱动因素，同时处理非线性关系、混合数据类型和私募市场普遍存在的缺失数据。

Result: 在包含约270,000家公司（其中超过53,000家有观测或可推导的投后估值）的全球私募市场数据集上，该框架在下游k近邻估值任务中优于传统的基于距离和文本嵌入的方法，同时保持基于案例的可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过估值视角学习公司相似性而非依赖静态特征或语义描述，能够更有效地识别经济意义上相似的同行公司，提升私募市场估值任务的准确性，并保留可解释性。

Abstract: As more investors contemplate private markets and contend with limited transparency, sparse disclosures, and infrequent transactions, identifying economically meaningful peer companies for comparison is a fundamental challenge for valuation, due diligence, portfolio construction, and risk management. We propose an ensemble tree-based supervised similarity learning framework that defines company similarity through the lens of market valuation rather than static feature matching or semantic descriptions. Specifically, we train a CatBoost gradient-boosted decision tree model on observed private company valuations and derive a valuation-aware similarity metric from importance-weighted leaf-node co-occurrences across the ensemble. The similarity metric captures shared valuation drivers while accommodating nonlinear relationships, mixed data types, and pervasive missing data common in private markets. Using a global private-market universe of approximately 270,000 companies, including more than 53,000 firms with observed or derivable post-money valuations spanning multiple industries, geographies, and deal stages, we demonstrate that the proposed similarity framework improves upon traditional distance-based and text-embedding-based approaches in downstream k-nearest-neighbor valuation tasks in the evaluated industry groups, while retaining case-based explainability.

</details>


### [185] [The Price of Permission: Classification Uncertainty in Constrained Capital Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12634)
*Abdulrahman Qadi,Akash Sharma,Francesca Medda*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究伊斯兰教法合规股票筛选中的分类不确定性（classification uncertainty），即投资者基础可能因标准分歧或接近合规边界而碎片化或即将改变的风险。通过1999-2024年CRSP-Compustat面板中13,188只证券、七种研究者模拟的Shariah规则手册，作者发现筛选规则分歧和临近活跃边界可预测下月屏幕隐含的转换。马来西亚证券委员会官方名单的核心事件证据显示，410次（剔除交易量阈值后295次）纳入事件在[0,10]和[0,20]交易日窗口产生显著正向匹配收益（1.76和2.25个百分点），但不存在无条件的平等加权许可溢价，且2023年DJIM/S&P方法变更没有稳健的匹配重新定价。作者结论是，官方Shariah许可在流动性足够的证券中与价格效应相关，但单纯的形式资格不足；分类风险应被视为投资组合监控状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰教法合规股票筛选提供了一个制度规则决定谁可以持有股票的透明环境。现有的二元标签只能指示当前资格，不能反映可行投资者基础是否在标准间碎片化或接近改变。作者将这种不稳定性定义为分类不确定性，并希望通过投资者许可质量（permitted investor mass）形式化其投资者基础后果，以理解分类风险对资产定价的影响。

Method: 使用1999-2024年CRSP-Compustat面板中13,188只证券，在七种研究者模拟的Shariah规则手册下进行分类。通过筛选规则分歧和接近活跃边界来排名预测下月屏幕隐含转换。采用Fama-MacBeth诊断测试无条件平等加权许可溢价，并在2023年DJIM/S&P方法变更时进行匹配重新定价测试。核心事件证据使用25份马来西亚证券委员会官方名单，对410个（剔除前期交易量门槛后295个）纳入事件计算匹配收益，并做留一日、首次纳入和期中安慰剂检验，同时进行所有权和需求压力诊断。

Result: 美国数据不支持无条件平等加权许可溢价，2023年DJIM/S&P方法变更无稳健匹配重新定价。马来西亚官方名单证据：410个纳入事件的正向但不精确的匹配收益；剔除交易量门槛后295个纳入在[0,10]交易日产生1.76个百分点（p_date=0.008；p_wild=0.017），在[0,20]窗口产生2.25个百分点（p_date=0.018；p_wild=0.035）。留一日、首次纳入和期中检查支持结论，但联合20日事件前检验拒绝。所有权和需求压力诊断未能识别唯一的边际买家或清晰的外生需求冲击。

Conclusion: 证据支持将分类风险视为投资组合监控状态。官方Shariah许可在流动性足够的证券（本地市场）中与价格效应相关，但单纯的形式资格不足以驱动定价影响。分类不确定性的量化和监控对投资组合管理具有实际意义。

Abstract: Shariah-compliant equity screening provides a transparent setting in which institutional rules determine who may own a stock. A binary label identifies current eligibility but not whether the feasible investor base is fragmented across standards or close to changing. We define this instability as classification uncertainty and formalize its investor-base consequence through permitted investor mass. In a 1999-2024 CRSP-Compustat panel of 13,188 securities classified under seven researcher-emulated Shariah rulebooks, screening-rule disagreement and proximity to active boundaries rank next-month screen-implied transitions. U.S. Fama-MacBeth diagnostics do not support an unconditional equal-weighted permission premium, and a September 2023 DJIM/S&P methodology change produces no robust matched repricing. The central event evidence uses 25 official Securities Commission Malaysia lists. The 410 inclusions already trading before the preceding review have positive but imprecise matched returns. Applying the pre-event turnover floor yields 295 inclusions with 1.76 percentage points over $[0,10]$ trading days ($p_{\mathrm{date}}=0.008$; $p_{\mathrm{wild}}=0.017$) and 2.25 points over $[0,20]$ ($p_{\mathrm{date}}=0.018$; $p_{\mathrm{wild}}=0.035$). Leave-one-date-out, first-inclusion-only, and mid-review placebo checks are supportive, although a joint 20-day pre-event test rejects. Ownership and demand-pressure diagnostics do not identify a unique marginal buyer or clean causal demand shock. The evidence supports treating classification risk as a portfolio-monitoring state. Official Shariah permission is associated with price effects in a recognized local market among sufficiently tradable securities; formal eligibility alone is insufficient.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Excitation-Supervised Closed-Loop Self-Calibration and Target Seeking for an Unknown-Pose Range-Bearing Relay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12528)
*Yash Bagla*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个激发监督控制层，用于车辆通过未知位置和偏航的中继（range-bearing relay）寻找隐藏目标的问题。核心发现是轨迹扩展裕度S_v同时充当有限噪声种子精度界、局部向量方差分解和圆几何激发预算，并基于此设计了一个激发-重置控制器，在激发证书不足时重新触发探索运动，否则进行无限制的目标搜索。通过闭合回路仿真、蒙特卡洛比较、阈值消融和ROS 2/Gazebo软件在环实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作（arXiv:2608.09464）仅静态地说明了哪些车辆相对观测可以消除标定规格并使目标包全局可操作，但这种陈述是事后的——它只能对一个已存储的窗口进行分类。需要一个闭环层的实时监督机制，来决定车辆自身运动何时已使中继标定可信，以及当不可信时应该采取什么行动。

Method: 本文证明轨迹扩展裕度S_v同时具备三重角色：有限噪声种子精度界、局部向量方差分解、圆几何激发预算。基于此构建激发监督算法，当扩展证书不足时重新触发探索运动并将目标搜索输入投影到远离激发方向，否则继续无限制目标搜索。监督规则可在有限时间内获取所需激发（在显式采样假设下），阈值从期望标定精度水平而非启发式选择。

Result: 在100对配对试验中，固定基线在衰减率过快时，偏航RMSE从0.010升至0.065 rad，成功率降至56%；而监督方法始终保持偏航RMSE在0.0095到0.0191 rad之间，成功率100%。目标跟踪误差不受影响。实验包括闭环仿真、蒙特卡洛比较、阈值消融和带传感延迟的ROS 2/Gazebo软件在环实验。

Conclusion: 激发监督在固定调度衰减超过未知的充分激发时间时至关重要。通过将阈值与期望标定精度水平挂钩，该方法避免了启发式调参，同时保证了在无噪声局部区域且正激发衰减下，估计器收敛能够带来目标搜索收敛。

Abstract: A vehicle seeking a hidden target through a range-bearing relay of unknown position and yaw must decide, online, whether its own motion has already made the relay calibration trustworthy, and what to do when it has not. Two distinct vehicle-relative observations are known to remove the calibration gauge and make the target's relay-local packet globally actionable (arXiv:2608.09464), but that statement is static: it classifies a stored window only after the fact. This paper supplies the closed-loop layer: we show that the trajectory-spread margin $S_v$ that governs identifiability is simultaneously a finite-noise seed-accuracy bound, a local-vector variance decomposition, and a circle-geometry excitation budget, and we use it to supervise an excitation-reset controller. An excitation-supervised algorithm retriggers exploratory motion whenever the spread certificate is insufficient, projecting the target-seeking input away from the excitation's push, and otherwise proceeds to unrestricted target seeking. Under explicit sampling assumptions the supervision rule provably acquires any required excitation in finite time; in the noiseless local regime with positive excitation decay, estimator convergence yields target-seeking convergence after certification; and the threshold is selected from a desired calibration-accuracy level rather than chosen heuristically. Closed-loop simulation, paired Monte Carlo comparisons, a spread-threshold ablation, and a ROS 2/Gazebo software-in-the-loop experiment with sensing delay validate the approach. A decay-rate sweep shows that supervision matters when a fixed schedule's decay outruns the unknown time-to-adequate-excitation: over 100 paired trials the fixed baseline's yaw RMSE rises from 0.010 to 0.065 rad and success falls to 56%, while target-tracking error remains insensitive; supervision keeps yaw RMSE between 0.0095 and 0.0191 rad with 100% success.

</details>


### [187] [The $θ$-Symmetric SRG with Applications to Stability of Cactus Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12591)
*Xiaokan Yang,Wei Chen,Li Qiu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了θ对称缩放相对图（SRG）并将其应用于仙人掌网络的稳定性分析。与传统SRG相比，θ对称SRG能表征相位超前/滞后行为，是多变量奈奎斯特图的自然推广。作者分别分析了其增益和相位特性，建立了θ分段相位与范数最小化问题之间的联系，从而可通过半定规划计算。进一步推导了子乘性和子加性性质，用于判定乘积型和求和型回差矩阵的非奇异性，进而为循环和平行反馈极端情况下的仙人掌网络建立鲁棒稳定性的充要条件，最终综合出统一的多回路仙人掌网络稳定性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缩放相对图（SRG）在描述系统行为时存在局限性，无法表征相位超前与滞后行为，且缺乏多变量情况下的直观几何解释。作者旨在提出一种更自然的、类似于经典奈奎斯特图的多变量扩展工具，并用于分析和综合仙人掌网络（cactus networks）的稳定性。

Method: 1) 定义θ对称SRG，分离分析其增益和相位特性；2) 建立θ分段相位与范数最小化问题的联系，利用半定规划计算相位；3) 推导θ对称SRG的子乘性和子加性代数性质；4) 基于这些性质判定乘积型和求和型回差矩阵的非奇异性；5) 以循环互联为起点建立鲁棒稳定性的充要条件，并与平行情况整合，构建统一的多回路仙人掌网络稳定性框架。

Result: 提出了一种新颖的θ对称SRG框架，成功实现了对仙人掌网络（包括循环和平行反馈极端情形）的鲁棒稳定性充要条件刻画。相比现有方法，该框架保守性更低，提供了更直观的几何解释。通过多个示例验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: θ对称SRG是一种比传统SRG更强大、更自然的多变量系统分析工具，能够为广义仙人掌网络提供统一且保守性更低的稳定性分析框架，同时兼具清晰的几何直观性。

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically study a variant of the scaled relative graph (SRG), referred to as the $θ$-symmetric SRG, and apply it to the stability analysis of cactus networks. Compared with the previous SRG definition, the $θ$-symmetric SRG enables the characterization of phase lead and lag behaviors, and serves as a more natural multivariable extension of the classical Nyquist plot. We first analyze the gain and phase aspects of $θ$-symmetric SRG separately and build a connection between $θ$-segmental phase and a norm minimization problem. This connection makes it possible to compute $θ$-segmental phase via semidefinite programming. We further derive the submultiplicative and subadditive properties of $θ$-symmetric SRG. These algebraic properties are crucial to determine the nonsingularity of product-type and sum-type return difference matrices, which topologically correspond to the cyclic and parallel-feedback extreme cases of cactus networks. By taking the cyclic interconnection as the fundamental starting point, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions for its robust stability. Integrating this with the parallel case, we synthesize a unified stability framework for general multi-loop cactus networks. The $θ$-symmetric SRG framework is less conservative and provides a more intuitive geometric interpretation of system behaviors compared with some existing approaches. Several examples are included to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [188] [Security-Constrained Operation of IBR-Dominated Power Systems: Static and Dynamic Security Across Preventive and Corrective Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12609)
*Buxin She*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文以"静态与动态安全"和"预防与校正决策时机"两个维度为框架，系统综述了基于逆变器资源(IBR)重塑安全约束运行的新格局，提出通用模型来表达预防-校正权衡，并指出IBR能力可扩大可行域或缓解安全约束，但需确保其在运行中可交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBR将电力系统运行与快速动态和控制依赖响应耦合，其可配置能力正在重塑安全约束运行的制定与协调方式，但现有文献缺乏统一的框架来组织和理解这一转变。

Method: 提出二维视角：静态vs动态安全、预防vs校正决策时机；构建通用公式显式表达预防-校正权衡；在此框架内系统综述并综合现有公式、方法和IBR能力表征。

Result: 两个发现：(1) IBR能力可扩大可行集或缓解安全约束，从而降低运行成本或提升安全性能；(2) 跨公式共享的能力和约束决定了该能力在运行中是否可交付。据此提出未来研究方向：IBR能力表征与量化、联合调度及可扩展求解框架。

Conclusion: IBR能力在安全约束运行中具有潜力和局限性：一方面能带来经济和安全的双重收益，另一方面其可交付性取决于公式间的共享约束。未来需进一步探索能力量化、联合调度与可扩展求解方法。

Abstract: Inverter-based resources (IBRs) couple power system operation to fast dynamics and controller-dependent responses. Their configurable capabilities are reshaping the formulation and coordination of security-constrained operation. This paper presents a two-axis view: static versus dynamic security and preventive versus corrective decision timing. Preventive scheduling is extending from static post-contingency feasibility toward dynamic security, while corrective operation spans equilibrium-based corrective actions and trajectory-based control. A generic formulation represents this change and makes the preventive--corrective tradeoff explicit. Existing formulations, methods, and capability representations are reviewed and synthesized within this framework. The surveyed work yields two findings. First, IBR capability can expand the feasible set or relieve security constraints, reducing operating cost or improving security performance. Second, shared capability and constraints across formulations determine whether that capability remains operationally deliverable. These findings motivate future research in IBR capability characterization and quantification, joint scheduling, and scalable solution frameworks.

</details>


### [189] [Improving Fast Charging Safety With Core Temperature Estimation Via Kolmogorov-Arnold Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12638)
*Faysal Ahamed,Tanushree Roy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）估算的电池核心温度融入鲁棒控制障碍函数（KAN-rCBF）约束的锂电池快速充电框架，通过求解带安全约束的二次规划问题，在KAN估计误差和模型不确定性下保证电池热安全，同时保持与现有方法相当的充电时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂电池快速充电会导致显著温升，对电池安全和寿命构成严重风险。然而电池核心温度在实际中无法直接测量，使得实时热安全保障极具挑战性。现有方法无法同时兼顾热安全保证与充电效率。

Method: 利用Kolmogorov-Arnold Network（KAN）基于电池表面温度、冷却液温度、冷却液功率和充电电流等可测信号估算电池核心温度，并将估算结果融入鲁棒控制障碍函数（rCBF）约束中，构建一个带安全约束的二次规划（QP）问题来求解最优充电策略。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能够在维持电池温度安全的同时，实现与最先进方法相当的充电时间；相比之下，现有最先进方法无法保证同等水平的热安全性。

Conclusion: 本文为电池快速充电提供了具有解析安全保证的实时热管理策略，有效应对了核心温度不可测和模型不确定性的挑战，在充电效率与热安全之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Fast charging of Lithium-ion batteries can lead to a significant temperature rise, which can cause serious risks to battery safety and lifetime. To ensure safe battery operation, thermal constraints must be enforced during the fast charging process. However, the core temperature of the battery cannot be directly measured in practice, which makes real-time safety enforcement challenging. This paper proposes a framework that incorporates core temperature estimates from Kolmogorov-Arnold Network within robust control barrier function (KAN-rCBF) constraints for battery fast-charging. The algorithm utilizes measurements from battery surface temperature, coolant temperature, coolant power, and charging current to solve a quadratic programming problem under safety constraints. We prescribe analytical safety guarantees for this optimal charging policy under KAN estimation errors and model uncertainty. Simulation results show that the proposed method maintains a safe battery temperature while achieving charging times comparable to the state-of-the-art method, where the latter fails to guarantee the same level of thermal safety.

</details>


### [190] [Time Distribution Principle Using Measured Traveling Waves in Power Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12730)
*Haozong Wang,Yayu Yang,Jiahui Yang,Muhamman Umar Afzaal,Yuru Wu,Yan Wen,Zhengfa Zhang,Yilu Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于行波（TW）测量的新型时间同步原理，利用电网中前向和后向行波传播的固有对称性，在不依赖外部时间参考的情况下实现高精度时间分配。通过在改进的IEEE 14节点系统上的电磁暂态仿真验证，该方法在正常条件下可实现微秒级同步精度，为分布式系统提供了一种更安全、对通信质量依赖更低的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间同步方法（如卫星同步和基于通信的方法）存在信号易受攻击、依赖通信延迟对称性等问题，迫切需要一种更安全、更可靠且不依赖外部参考的时间同步方法。

Method: 提出基于行波测量的时间同步原理，利用电力系统中前向与后向行波传播的固有对称性来实现时间同步，无需外部时间参考；通过电磁暂态仿真在改进的IEEE 14节点系统中进行验证。

Result: 在正常条件下，所提方法实现了微秒级的时间同步精度，证明了基于行波的时间同步原理的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: 基于行波测量的时间同步原理有望成为传统方法的替代方案，能够提供更高的安全性和更低的通信质量依赖，适用于分布式系统的高精度时间同步需求。

Abstract: Accurate time synchronization is essential for distributed systems. Conventional methods, such as satellite-based synchronization and communication-based approaches, face challenges including signal vulnerability and dependence on communication delay symmetry. This paper proposes a novel time synchronization principle based on traveling wave (TW) measurements in power grids. By leveraging the inherent symmetry of forward and backward TW propagation, the proposed method achieves high-precision time distribution without relying on external time references. The methodology is validated through electromagnetic transient simulations on a modified IEEE 14-bus system. The results demonstrate that under normal conditions, the proposed approach achieves microsecond-level synchronization accuracy. These findings suggest that the TW-based time synchronization principle is a potential alternative to traditional methods, offering improved security and reduced dependence on communication quality.

</details>


### [191] [Technical Report on Resilient and Secure Large-Scale Energy Internet Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12916)
*Ioannis Zografopoulos,Karen Largman,Isaac Ortega Romero,S M Zia Ur Rashid,Yexiang Chen,George Fragkos,Charalambos Konstantinou,Subhash Lakshminarayana,Juan Ospina,Airin Rahman,Suman Rath,Vivek Kumar Singh,Mucun Sun,Wei Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本报告由IEEE PES工作组撰写，系统审视了大规模能源互联网（EI）系统的安全性与韧性。EI将电力、信息和市场三层紧密耦合并通过深度数字化互联。报告刻画了EI的网络物理威胁态势，综述了检测、保障与缓解技术，提出了捕获网络物理相互依赖的建模、控制与决策框架（涵盖储能集成、多维韧性和电价预测），考察了人工智能的对抗风险与可信部署，并引入了基于图、具备攻击韧性的信息路由方法。最后提出研究、标准化与监管建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模能源互联网深度融合电力、信息与市场层，数字化带来便利的同时也引入了复杂的网络物理安全威胁与韧性挑战，亟需系统性审视威胁态势并整合现有技术框架以指导未来研究与部署。

Method: 作为IEEE PES工作组报告，采用综合性综述与框架调研方法：刻画网络物理威胁态势，综述检测/保障/缓解技术，提出建模、控制与决策框架，评估AI对抗风险与可信部署，并引入基于图论的攻击韧性信息路由方案。

Result: 系统呈现了EI网络物理威胁全貌，整合了含储能集成、多维韧性与电价预测的相互依赖建模框架，评估了AI可信部署风险，提出了图基攻击韧性路由方法。

Conclusion: 实现大规模EI的安全与韧性需在科研、标准化与监管三方面协同推进，本报告为此提供了系统性的技术全景与行动建议。

Abstract: This IEEE PES Task Force report examines the security and resilience of large-scale Energy Internet (EI) systems, in which electricity, information, and market layers are tightly coupled through pervasive digitalization. The report characterizes the EI cyber-physical threat landscape and surveys detection, assurance, and mitigation techniques, presents modeling, control, and decision-making frameworks that capture cyber-physical interdependencies, including storage integration, multi-dimensional resilience, and electricity price forecasting, examines adversarial risks and trustworthy deployment of artificial intelligence, and introduces graph-based, attack-resilient information routing. The report closes with recommendations for research, standardization, and regulatory efforts needed to realize a resilient and secure large-scale EI.

</details>


### [192] [AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning for Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13083)
*Sajedeh Norouzi,Maryam Ansarifard,Farshad Zeinali,Ali Nouruzi,Nader Mokari,Hamid Saeedi,Nizar Zorba*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AGDRP（AoI保证的动态路径规划）的新型双因素路径规划方案，将行驶时间估计与无线电资源可用性（通过信息年龄AoI衡量）相结合，并采用深度强化学习（DRL）来解决这一双目标优化问题，实验表明其性能优于仅优化行驶时间的传统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态路径规划仅关注路线容量和行驶时间等指标，忽略了车载通信中无线电资源可用性的影响。随着智能交通系统和联网车辆（CVs）的发展，需要在路径规划中同时考虑通信质量（如信息新鲜度/AoI）与行驶效率，以提升整体系统性能。

Method: 提出一个双因素（dual-factor）路径规划框架，同时将行驶时间估计和无线电资源可用性（以AoI衡量）纳入路径规划目标。采用深度强化学习（DRL）作为求解算法，通过自适应学习来动态平衡行驶时间与AoI两个优化目标，实现随时间演化的动态路径规划。

Result: 仿真结果表明，AGDRP方案在路径规划性能上优于仅聚焦行驶时间优化的基线方案。将AoI最小化纳入路径规划可以显著提升整体路径规划性能，超越了传统的基于行驶时间的方法。

Conclusion: 将通信信息新鲜度（AoI）与行驶时间联合优化可以有效提升联网车辆的路径规划性能，DRL是解决此类双目标动态路径规划问题的有效工具，为智能交通系统中的车路协同路径规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: The advancement of Intelligent Transportation Sys- tems (ITS) has been significantly driven by progress in radio communication technology. Dynamic route planning, a key com- ponent of ITS, traditionally focuses on metrics such as route capacity and travel time. This paper presents a novel dual- factor approach that integrates travel time estimation and radio resource availability into an innovative route-planning scheme for connected vehicles (CVs). To address this dual-objective route planning challenge, we employ Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL). Our approach, called AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning (AGDRP), effectively balances travel time and Age of Information (AoI), enhancing route planning performance through adaptive learning over time. Simulation results demon- strate that AGDRP outperforms the baseline scheme, which solely focuses on travel time optimization. In fact, we show that incor- porating AoI minimization significantly enhances route planning performance beyond conventional travel-time-based approaches.

</details>


### [193] [Robust Space-Filling Input Design via Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13360)
*Máté Kiss,Roland Tóth,Maarten Schoukens*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒的填充空间输入设计算法，能够为整个模型类别生成空间填充数据集，通过期望最优性准则和随机逼近技术优化，解决了传统方法因假设模型偏差而导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的空间填充输入设计方法依赖于一个假定的系统模型，当真实系统与假设模型存在显著差异时，设计出的信号性能会远低于最优。因此需要一种对模型不确定性具有鲁棒性的设计方法。

Method: 该方法对模型类别总体上的最优性准则取期望，并利用随机逼近技术来优化这一鲁棒准则，从而生成覆盖整个模型类别的空间填充数据集。

Result: 通过仿真示例验证了所提出算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该鲁棒空间填充输入设计算法克服了对假设模型的依赖，能够在模型类别不确定的情况下仍生成性能良好的空间填充数据集。

Abstract: The space-filling input design approach generates a so-called space-filling dataset in the feature space of the system model. The design method is applicable on a broad class of model structures with wide selection of signals and also incorporates information measures through optimality criteria into the signal design. However, during the signal design, knowledge of a hypothesized model is required. The designed signal can perform far from the optimal if the true system is significantly different from the hypothesized system model. This paper proposes a robust space-filling input design algorithm that can generate a space-filling dataset for an entire class of models. The proposed algorithm takes the expectation of an optimality criterion over the population of the model class, and a stochastic approximation technique is employed to optimize this robust criteria. The efficiency of the proposed algorithm is demonstrated in a simulation example.

</details>


### [194] [Process-Aware Cross-Layer Adaptation for O-RAN-Enabled Industrial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13372)
*Elahe Delavari,Junaid Farooq,M. Majid Butt,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种进程感知的协同自适应引擎（PACE）框架，该框架结合应用结果、过程状态、无线遥测、边缘计算状态和感知配置，为闭环工业无线网络应用选择跨完整链路的协同运行点。通过工厂检测案例研究（集成物理数字孪生、可编程5G O-RAN网络和边缘视觉推理）验证，结果表明资源分配随生产速度变化而变化，独立调整各组件可能低效，且少量全系统评估即可识别高效配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无线网络性能指标（吞吐量、延迟、推理精度）单独衡量无法判断闭环工业应用是否完成了有用任务，因为数据从感知到处理再到行动的完整闭环中，各组件需要协同优化，而传统方法缺乏对整个过程和物理状态的感知。

Method: 设计PACE框架，联合考虑应用结果、物理过程状态、无线遥测、边缘计算状态和感知配置五个维度，通过协同选择整个闭环（感知-传输-计算-执行）上的运行点来实现资源优化。在工厂检测场景中采用物理数字孪生、可编程5G O-RAN网络和边缘视觉推理进行实证评估。

Result: 实验表明：1）最优资源分配方案随生产速度变化而动态改变；2）独立地调整系统各组件（而非协同调整）可能导致效率低下；3）只需相对较少的全系统评估次数即可识别出高效配置方案。

Conclusion: 闭环工业应用需要进程感知的端到端协同优化框架，跨网络、计算和感知层联合调优比独立优化各组件更有效，且该优化可以在较少的系统评估内完成。

Abstract: Wireless networks increasingly support closed-loop industrial applications in which sensed data must be transmitted, processed, and converted into an action before physical process makes the result obsolete. Throughput, latency, and inference accuracy measured separately cannot determine whether such an application completed a useful task. We propose a Process-Aware Co-adaptation Engine framework that combines application outcomes, process state, radio telemetry, edge-compute state, and sensing configuration to select coordinated operating points across the complete loop. We evaluate the proposed approach in a factory-inspection case study that integrates a physics-based digital twin, a programmable 5G O-RAN network, and edge-based visual inference. The experiments show that the preferred resource allocation changes with production speed and that adapting individual system components independently can be inefficient. We further show that efficient configurations can be identified with relatively few full-system evaluations.

</details>


### [195] [AaLLM: An End-to-End Analog Circuit Design Framework from Topology Generation to Sizing Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13472)
*Mohammed Ayman Habib,Rylan Hart,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出AaLLM，一个开源端到端的多智能体LLM工作流，自动从用户规格生成电路网表，涵盖拓扑生成和电路尺寸优化。通过自动化知识库构建、RAG模型和创新的三智能体反馈系统（Designer、Critic、Evaluator），在保证性能的同时显著减少了迭代次数和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模拟电路设计依赖专家直觉且耗时费力；现有LLM方法仅提供碎片化解决方案（只关注尺寸或拓扑），需要手动添加技术知识，易产生幻觉，无法创造创新拓扑，且迭代过程繁琐低效。

Method: AaLLM采用多智能体架构：1) 自动从论文和教科书中构建知识库，替代手动数据收集；2) 使用RAG模型模拟电路设计专业知识；3) 提出新颖的三智能体反馈系统——Designer确定电路元件值，Critic审查这些值，Evaluator通过仲裁前两者来最小化电路尺寸迭代。

Result: AaLLM生成的创新拓扑在FoM指标上与已知拓扑相当，某些电路提升高达3倍。与SOTA多智能体LLM管线相比，推理时SPICE调用次数减少3-4.5倍，墙钟时间减少40倍。

Conclusion: AaLLM证明了端到端多智能体LLM工作流在模拟电路设计中的有效性，不仅能够生成创新拓扑，还大幅提高了设计效率，为电路自动化设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Analog circuit design is a time-consuming, iterative process in a nonlinear and high-dimensional design space that relies heavily on expert intuition. Among recent developments, LLMs have introduced a promising approach by bringing natural language reasoning to circuit design tasks. The majority of conventional LLM-based approaches provide fragmented solutions that focus either only on sizing or topology generation. These methods require adding specific technical knowledge manually, which is inefficient and prone to hallucinations during circuit sizing. Moreover, the inherent trade-off in meeting different specs makes current approaches iterative and tedious. Another shortcoming is the inability to create innovative topologies, which may lead to sub-optimal designs due to reliance on conventional topologies. In this paper, we present AaLLM, an open-source end-to-end multi-agent LLM workflow that takes user specs as input and outputs the appropriate netlist, encompassing both topology generation and circuit sizing. AaLLM automates the creation of a relevant knowledge base from research papers and textbooks to combat tedious manual data collection. A RAG model is implemented to emulate circuit design expertise using this knowledge base. Moreover, AaLLM uses a novel tri-agent feedback system comprising a Designer that determines circuit component values, a Critic that scrutinizes these values, and an Evaluator that minimizes circuit sizing iterations by arbitrating between the other two agents. AaLLM-generated novel topologies achieve a figure of merit (FoM) comparable to that of known topologies, and up to 3x higher for certain circuits. Testing on several circuit topologies, our results show a 3x - 4.5x decrease in the number of SPICE calls at inference when compared to SOTA multi-agent LLM pipelines. The results also show a 40x decrease in wall-clock time compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [196] [Safety-Critical Control for Quadrotor UAVs via Decentralized Navigation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13507)
*Omayra Yago Nieto,Alexandre Anahory Simoes,Leonardo Colombo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究四旋翼无人机编队在学习的模型不确定性下，由分散导航函数驱动的安全关键控制问题。针对四旋翼仅能沿机体垂直轴产生推力的约束，构建了推力-姿态实现方案并量化了其相对全驱动参考动力学的误差。通过聚合鲁棒HOCBF-QP安全滤波器，以最小化修改名义推力的方式保证无人机对间碰撞避免的高概率满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机编队运行面临两大挑战：一是实际执行器约束（仅能产生沿机体垂直轴的推力）与全驱动导航函数生成的参考力不匹配；二是模型学习存在不确定性，影响安全性保证。需要一个能够在考虑执行器约束和模型不确定性的前提下，同时保证多无人机对间碰撞避免的安全关键控制框架。

Method: 1) 构造推力-姿态实现，将全驱动平移参考力映射为四旋翼实际可执行的推力与姿态指令，并量化该映射误差；2) 提出聚合鲁棒HOCBF-QP（高阶控制障碍函数-二次规划）安全滤波器，对名义推力进行最小化修改；3) 在模型不确定性下以高概率保证成对碰撞避免。

Result: 所提出的框架在模型不确定性和四旋翼欠驱动动力学约束下，能够以高概率保证无人机间的碰撞避免，同时实现对名义推力指令的最小化偏离。推力-姿态实现的误差被显式量化并纳入安全滤波器的鲁棒设计。

Conclusion: 该工作将分散导航函数、推力-姿态动力学实现与鲁棒HOCBF-QP安全过滤相结合，为存在学习模型不确定性的大规模四旋翼编队提供了具有概率安全保证的控制解决方案。

Abstract: We study safety-critical control for teams of quadrotor UAVs driven by decentralized navigation functions under learned model uncertainty. These functions generate fully actuated translational reference forces, while quadrotors can only produce thrust along their body-fixed vertical axes. We construct a thrust-attitude implementation of the induced navigation forces and quantify its error with respect to the fully actuated reference dynamics. An aggregated robust HOCBF-QP safety filter minimally modifies the nominal thrusts while guaranteeing pairwise collision avoidance with high probability.

</details>


### [197] [Joint Communication-Control Strategy Optimization with Partially Nested Information Structures: The Linear-Quadratic Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13535)
*Haoyi You,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多智能体线性系统中联合通信-控制策略优化（JCCO）问题，在分散随机控制的公共信息（CIB）框架下，针对部分嵌套（PN）信息结构，提出了保持部分嵌套性的条件，并开发了基于动态规划的算法来计算最优控制策略，得到闭式Riccati方程，并扩展到闭环通信策略场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，通信策略与控制策略通常需要联合优化（JCCO问题），但该问题在计算上具有挑战性。已有工作缺乏在部分嵌套信息结构下对JCCO问题的系统性形式化处理，以及保证线性最优策略的条件分析。

Method: 在CIB框架下，以基线通信协议产生PN信息结构为出发点，建立保持部分嵌套性的一系列条件；在此基础上，开发基于动态规划的方法计算JCCO在开环通信策略下的最优控制策略，获得闭式Riccati方程；并将该方法扩展到闭环通信策略场景。

Result: 建立了PN信息结构在通信策略优化下得以保持的条件（违反可能导致最优策略非线性）；得到了JCCO开环通信策略下的闭式Riccati方程；提供了一种求解CIB框架下带输出反馈的PN分布式LQ控制的新途径；闭环通信策略下得到比无限维CIB-信念动态规划更易处理的动态规划。

Conclusion: 本文为多智能体线性系统中联合通信与控制策略优化提供了系统的理论框架和计算高效的方法，涵盖开环与闭环通信策略，并给出了PN信息结构保持的条件及相应的闭式解。

Abstract: In this paper, we formalize a joint communication-control strategy optimization (JCCO) problem in multi-agent linear systems with quadratic costs, under the common-information-based (CIB) framework from decentralized stochastic control. For computational tractability, we focus on such JCCO problems with partially nested (PN) information structures (ISs). In particular, with a baseline communication protocol that leads to a PN IS, we establish a series of conditions under which the partial nestedness is preserved under the (additional) communication strategies to be optimized, while violating them may cause nonlinearity of the optimal strategies in general, with open-loop communication strategies. We then develop a dynamic-programming-based approach to compute the optimal control strategies of JCCO with open-loop communication strategies, which yields a set of closed-form Riccati Equations. As a byproduct of independent interest, such an approach also offers a way to solve decentralized linear-quadratic control with PN ISs and output feedback, under the CIB framework. Finally, we extend such an approach to JCCOs with closed-loop communication strategies, yielding a more tractable dynamic program than an infinite-dimensional CIB-belief-based one.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Supervised Mixed-Frequency Learning for Macro-Financial Forecasting When Factors are Weak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12589)
*Ulrich Hounyo,Zhendong Li*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出SsPCA-MIDAS方法，将监督式缩放主成分分析（SsPCA）整合进混频数据抽样（MIDAS）框架，用于在弱因子环境下进行低频目标的预测，并证明其在一致性和渐近正态性方面的理论性质，实证表明其在宏观经济金融预测中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Factor-MIDAS回归依赖主成分分析提取高频预测变量的公共因子，但PCA假设因子具有渗透性（pervasiveness），而宏观金融预测中常见的弱因子往往会违反这一假设，导致预测性能下降。因此，需要一个在弱因子条件下依然稳健的预测框架。

Method: 将监督式缩放PCA（SsPCA）集成到MIDAS混频抽样框架中，形成SsPCA-MIDAS方法。该方法通过利用预测目标的信息来缩放主成分，从而更有效地提取与预测目标相关的因子。论文还建立了弱因子条件下的一致性和渐近正态性理论，并将机器学习技术（如boosting）应用于提取的清洁因子以进一步提升性能。

Result: 模拟实验表明SsPCA-MIDAS在弱因子普遍存在的情况下优于基于PCA和其他监督方法的竞争者。将boosting等机器学习技术应用于提取的因子可带来额外收益。在美国宏观金融预测的广泛应用中，SsPCA-MIDAS选择了具有经济意义的预测因子，并显著改善了GDP、通胀、失业率、资产价格和波动率的预测。

Conclusion: SsPCA-MIDAS为弱因子环境下的混频预测提供了一个理论上可靠且实践有效的解决方案，弥补了传统Factor-MIDAS的不足，并展示了与机器学习技术结合的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Factor-MIDAS regressions forecast a low-frequency target by extracting common factors from a large panel of high-frequency predictors via principal component analysis (PCA). While PCA mitigates the curse of dimensionality, it relies on factor pervasiveness, an assumption often violated when factors are weak, as is common in macro-financial forecasting. We propose SsPCA-MIDAS, which integrates supervised scaled PCA (SsPCA) into the mixed-data sampling framework. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality under weak factors, permitting inference on the prediction target. Simulations show that SsPCA-MIDAS outperforms competing PCA-based and supervised methods, especially when weak factors are prevalent. Applying machine-learning techniques such as boosting to the cleaner factors it extracts yields further gains. An extensive application to U.S. macro-financial forecasting shows that SsPCA-MIDAS selects economically meaningful predictors and improves forecasts of GDP, inflation, unemployment, asset prices, and volatility.

</details>


### [199] [Estimation of distribution functions, their jumps and interval probabilities under measurement error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13152)
*Kairat Mynbaev,Carlos Martins-Filho,Chad Brown*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文针对经典加性测量误差模型 X=Y+Z，提出直接估计潜在变量Y分布函数F_Y的三个泛函（连续性点处的分布函数、区间概率、预指定不连续点处的跳跃大小）的新方法。通过连接傅里叶反演定理与Mynbaev等人(2022)提出的估计器代数结构，该方法不要求F_Y具有密度、混合表示或全局Sobolev光滑性假设，适用于任意潜在分布（含离散、连续分量及多重跳跃）。论文给出了非渐近偏差与方差界，建立了渐近无偏性和一致性，并通过模拟研究评估了可行调参程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测量误差模型估计方法通常要求潜在分布F_Y具有密度函数、混合表示或满足全局光滑性假设，这在许多实际场景中过于严格。作者旨在放宽这些限制，使得估计方法能够适应任意潜在分布，包括含离散与连续分量、存在多重跳跃的复杂分布。

Method: 作者建立了傅里叶反演定理与Mynbaev, Martins-Filho和Henderson(2022)提出的估计器类代数结构之间的联系，据此构建直接估计器。针对F_Y的三个泛函（连续性点处的分布函数、区间概率、预指定不连续点处跳跃大小）分别推导非渐近偏差与方差界，并证明其渐近无偏性和一致性。

Result: 论文推导了估计器的非渐近偏差和方差界，建立了渐近无偏性和一致性。与之前工作相比，该方法不需要F_Y具有密度、混合表示或全局Sobolev光滑性假设，框架可容纳任意潜在分布。模拟研究评估了可行的调参程序，并在可用时与现有方法进行了有限样本性能比较。

Conclusion: 该研究为测量误差模型中的分布估计提供了更具普遍性的理论框架，放宽了现有方法的严格要求，使估计器适用于更广泛的真实场景（如存在离散或混合分量的分布），并通过模拟验证了方法的实用性和有限样本表现。

Abstract: We consider the classical additive measurement-error model $X=Y+Z$, where the latent random variable $Y$ has unknown distribution $F_Y$ and the error $Z$ has a known distribution. We develop direct estimators for three functionals of $F_Y$: (i) $F_Y(x)$ at continuity points; (ii) interval probabilities $F_Y(y)-F_Y(x)$ when $x<y$ are continuity points; and (iii) the size of a jump at a prespecified discontinuity. We derive non-asymptotic bias and variance bounds, and establish asymptotic unbiasedness and consistency. Unlike previous work, we do not require $F_Y$ to admit a density, have a mixture representation, or satisfy global Sobolev smoothness assumptions. The framework accommodates arbitrary latent distributions, including those with both discrete and continuous components, and distributions with multiple jumps. These results rely on a link between Fourier inversion theorems and the algebraic structure of a class of estimators proposed in Mynbaev, Martins-Filho and Henderson (2022). A simulation study evaluates feasible tuning procedures and, where available, compares the finite-sample performance of the proposed estimators with existing methods.

</details>


### [200] [Parameter Identification in Autoregressions under Discrete Sampling or Temporal Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13224)
*Marko Mlikota*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究了AR(p)过程在每q期观测一次时的可识别性（包括stock变量快照和flow变量区间求和两种采样方式）。在温和假设下，作者推导了一般滞后阶数p和采样频率q的识别集，界定了其基数，并提供计算所有候选点及判定其是否属于识别集的方法。作者提出并部分证明了一个猜想：误差项方差点可识别；时序聚合下自回归参数点可识别；离散采样下，奇采样频率时自回归参数点可识别，偶采样频率时仅在交替符号意义下可识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间聚合（temporal aggregation）和离散采样（discrete sampling）在计量经济学中极为常见，但现有文献对AR(p)过程在这种采样下的参数可识别性缺乏系统分析。作者旨在填补这一空白，为一般p和q设定下推导完整的识别集理论，并验证关于点可识别性的猜想。

Method: 作者在温和假设下推导AR(p)过程经过每q期观测后的识别集。具体包括：推导一般p∈N和q∈N下识别集的完整刻画；界定识别集的基数；提供计算所有候选点并判定其是否属于识别集的算法步骤。理论分析辅以数值验证来支持猜想。

Result: 作者给出并部分证明了以下猜想：(i) 误差项方差始终点可识别；(ii) 时序聚合（flow）下自回归参数点可识别；(iii) 离散采样（stock）下，奇采样频率时点可识别，偶采样频率时识别到交替符号（alternating sign）的程度。该猜想在部分设定下被严格证明，其他情况通过数值实验得到广泛验证。

Conclusion: 本文为时间聚合与离散采样下AR(p)过程的参数可识别性提供了系统而完整的理论框架，明确了哪些参数在何种采样条件下可识别程度如何，对实证研究中应用聚合数据的计量分析具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: I consider an AR($p$) process that is observed every $q$ periods, either as a snapshot (stock variable) or as a sum over the sampling interval (flow variable). Under fairly mild assumptions, I derive the identified set for general lag lengths $p \in \mathbb{N}$ and sampling frequencies $q \in \mathbb{N}$, I bound its cardinality, and I provide a recipe to compute all candidate points and determine their membership in the identified set. My analysis supports the following conjecture: (i) the error term-variance is point-identified, (ii) under temporal aggregation, the autoregressive parameters are point-identified, and (iii) under discrete sampling they are point-identified for odd sampling frequencies and identified up to alternating sign for even sampling frequencies. I prove this conjecture in some settings and verify it numerically more broadly.

</details>


### [201] [Measuring the Arrow of Time: Identification, Estimation, and Inference for Directional Structure in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13431)
*Avishek Bhandari*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套完整的方法来分析耦合时间序列的方向性（即哪个先动、动得多强），其核心思想是：方向恰好是系统行为中在时间反演下发生变化的部分。作者将方向形式化为滞后协方差所承载的循环矩阵（circulation matrix），其消失即线性系统的统计时间可逆性，并构建了交叉拟合估计器、delete-block jackknife标准误和随机化检验等完整推断工具，在多个基准系统中与相关网络、Granger因果、转移熵等方法对比，指出这些方法在度量方向时的失效之处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题都涉及观察多个耦合序列并判断哪个先动、哪些关系更强。现有工具（如相关矩阵、距离度量、生成树、无向中心性、主成分分析）仅基于同期协方差构建，无法携带任何方向信息——一个可逆系统和一个循环系统可以在同一时间记录下具有完全相同的协方差。因此需要一种能够真正度量方向性的方法。

Method: 作者将方向形式化为一由滞后协方差承载的循环矩阵（circulation matrix），其消失等价于线性系统的统计时间可逆性；通过特征映射（feature maps）将这一刻画推广到非线性系统；在高斯基准下，其大小是熵产生泛函（前向与反向记录之间单位时间发散度的二次分量）。在此基础上构建了消除一阶偏差的交叉拟合估计器、delete-block jackknife标准误、在指定块零假设下精确的随机化检验，并包含族系误差校正和非线性扩展。还提供了采样理论（何时箭头可测）以及设计层（区分传递与时钟排序）。

Result: 该方法在四个已知答案的基准系统中与相关网络、Granger因果关系、转移熵和连通性指数等方法进行比较，报告了每种方法在作为方向度量时各自的失效方式（包括作者自己的方法）。论文提供了完整的算法和两种语言的实现示例，作为一系列应用的基础参考。

Conclusion: 方向性是独立于同期协方差的信息维度，可以通过循环矩阵和熵产生函数被严格刻画和估计。本文提供的完整推断框架（估计、标准误、检验和设计层）为该方向的应用建立了基础。

Abstract: Many questions across the sciences take the same form: several coupled series are observed together, and the analyst wants to know not merely that they move together but which one moves first, and how strongly. This paper sets out a complete method built on one organising idea: the direction of a coupled system is exactly the part of its behaviour that changes when the record is played backwards. Tools built on contemporaneous covariance alone (correlation matrices, distance measures, spanning trees, undirected centralities, principal components) carry no information about direction: a reversible system and a circulating one can share identical covariance at every sampling of the same point-in-time record. Formally, direction is a circulation matrix carried by the lagged covariance. Its vanishing is exactly statistical time reversibility for linear systems, feature maps carry the characterisation to nonlinear ones, and under the Gaussian benchmark its magnitude is an entropy-production functional of the identified circulation, the quadratic component of the divergence per unit time between the forward and reversed records. Around this estimand we build a cross-fitted estimator removing first-order bias, delete-block jackknife standard errors, and a randomisation test exact under its stated block null, with a familywise correction and a nonlinear extension. A sampling theory says when the arrow is measurable at all, and a design layer separates transmission from the ordering of clocks. A laboratory of four systems with known answers compares the method with correlation networks, Granger causality, transfer entropy, and connectedness indices, reporting the failures of each when read as a measure of direction, including our own. Complete algorithms and worked examples in two languages make the paper the base reference for a series of applications.

</details>


### [202] [Learning about Treatment Effects in Panels under Unknown Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13466)
*Shengbin Wei*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在比较单元可能对处理做出反应（存在溢出效应）时，从面板结果中识别处理效应的方法。该方法不需要预先知道干扰模式或暴露映射，通过为每个凸捐赠者权重在预处理前拟合其有效性界限，并结合针对应用场景的预设限制，构建出尖锐的识别集。当附加限制具有有限线性表示时，验证提出的处理效应是否与模型兼容等价于求解有限线性系统，并通过自助法校准检验。将该方法应用于亚利桑那州法律工人法案，结果显示95%的倒置集在所有报告规格中包含两种符号的效应，无法确定处理效应的符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面板比较研究中，当比较单元也可能对处理做出反应时，观测到的面板结果同时反映处理效应和溢出效应。当干扰模式未知时，仅凭观测结果无法区分这两者。本文旨在刻画在这种一般约束下能从面板结果中学习到什么，而不需要暴露映射或对受影响捐赠者的事先分类。

Method: 该方法框架将每个凸捐赠者权重的有效性界限按其预处理前的拟合度进行缩放，并将这些界限与针对应用场景预设的限制相结合。有效性界限约束处理效应相对于溢出效应的关系，而附加限制决定其可能取值，两者共同产生尖锐的识别集。当附加限制具有有限线性表示时，通过检查有限线性系统是否有解来验证提出的处理效应是否与模型兼容，并采用自助法校准检验这一条件。

Result: 倒置这些检验在大样本中均匀控制错误排除每个兼容值的概率。在亚利桑那州法律工人法案的应用中，所得95%倒置集在所有报告规格中包含正负两种符号的效应，处理效应的符号问题未能解决。

Conclusion: 本文在干扰模式未知的泛化设定下，成功构建了处理效应的尖锐识别集，提供了一种可检验的框架。应用案例表明，在存在溢出效应的情况下，处理效应的符号可能无法确定，这凸显了该方法的实用价值和对处理效应推断不确定性的重要认识。

Abstract: When comparison units may also respond to treatment, panel comparisons reflect both the treatment effect and spillovers. If the interference pattern is unknown, observed outcomes alone do not separate the two. I characterize what can nevertheless be learned from panel outcomes under general restrictions, without requiring an exposure mapping or prior classification of affected donors. The framework scales validity bounds for every convex donor weight by its fit before treatment and combines these bounds with prespecified restrictions tailored to the application. The validity bounds constrain the treatment effect relative to spillovers, while the additional restrictions determine its possible values. Together these restrictions yield a sharp identified set. When the additional restrictions have a finite linear representation, checking whether a proposed treatment effect is compatible with the model reduces exactly to asking whether a finite linear system has a solution. Bootstrap calibration tests this condition. Inverting these tests uniformly controls, in large samples, the probability of falsely excluding each compatible value. In an application to the Legal Arizona Workers Act, the resulting 95 percent inversion sets contain effects of both signs across all reported specifications, leaving the sign of the treatment effect unresolved.

</details>

### [203] [AI-Driven Multiscenario Interest Rate Forecasting: A Proof of Concept for Banking Asset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12424)
*Ekkehardt Bauer,Dirk Holländer,Linus Wolff,Christoph Ostermair,Kyrillus Aiad,Joachim Hasebrook*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个AI支持的多视角利率预测原型系统，将经典计量经济学模型（以贝叶斯向量自回归BVAR为核心）与现代AI方法（主题建模、情感分析、市场分析）相结合，在欧洲一家大型银行进行了测试，为资产负债管理（ALM）中的战略决策提供更精确灵活的利率预测支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的利率预测方法往往依赖单一信息来源，难以全面捕捉货币政策趋势和情感信号。银行需要在利率风险管理中整合分散的信息源，以提高预测精度和决策质量，因此需要一种多视角、AI驱动的综合预测框架。

Method: 系统整合了主题建模、情感分析、计量经济学预测和市场分析，并集成于一个交互式平台中。核心计量模型为贝叶斯向量自回归（BVAR），支持基于模拟的情景分析，以多角度评估经济走势。AI用于分析大规模金融文档和市场数据，提前识别货币政策趋势和情感信号。

Result: 系统在欧洲一家主要银行得到测试验证，使财务分析师和风险管理师能够更准确地评估利率风险，更主动地管理市场波动。研究表明多视角AI驱动的预测为银行提供了实质性附加价值，提升了透明度、加强了基于证据的决策，并改善了风险管理。

Conclusion: 该原型展示了AI如何变革银行业的利率管理，但仍需进一步开发以优化实时数据集成和监管合规。尽管如此，研究已经表明多视角AI驱动的预测在银行业具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: This study focuses on developing an AI-supported prototype for multiperspective interest rate forecasting that combines classical econometric models with modern artificial intel-ligence methods. Tested in a major European bank, the system enables more precise and flexible prediction of interest rate developments, supporting strategic decision-making in Asset-Liability Management (ALM). It integrates topic modeling, sentiment analysis, econometric forecasting, and market-based analyses within an interactive platform. Leveraging AI to analyze large volumes of financial documents and market data enables the identification of monetary policy trends and sentiment signals at an early stage. The core econometric model is a Bayesian vector autoregression (BVAR) that enables simulation-based scenario analyses to evaluate economic developments from multiple perspectives. The system's innovation lies in its integration of several forecasting approaches that consolidate previously separate information sources and present them transparently and interpretably. Financial analysts and risk managers thus gain a better basis for making decisions, allowing them to assess interest rate risks more accurately and manage market movements more proactively. While the prototype demonstrates how AI can transform interest rate management in banking, further development is required to optimize real-time data integration and regulatory compliance. Even at this stage, the study shows that multi-perspective, AI-driven forecasting provides substantial added value for banks by increasing transparency, strengthening evidence-based decision-making, and improving risk management.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond the Skew-Stickiness Ratio: Transport Geometry of Spot-Driven Variance Surface Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12493)
*Charlie Che,Pradeepta Das*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无套利隐含波动率曲面动力学的几何理论，将微笑动态建模为可容许无静态套利曲面上的输运流。该框架通过输运速度场v(k)统一了所有经典粘性（stickiness）制度，并证明sticky-strike、sticky-delta、SSR、局部波动率和粗糙波动率均为其特例。基于五年SPX隐含波动率数据的实证研究表明：SSR系数从1个月期的1.44单调降至2年期的1.01（超偏斜行为）；自相似输运在所有期限被拒绝；速度曲线在中期随金钱度显著变化。三参数模型在中期期限的曲率动态上比SSR模型提升17-21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐含波动率曲面动态模型缺乏统一的几何框架，不同的粘性制度（sticky-strike、sticky-delta等）各自独立，无法在保证无套利约束的前提下统一描述微笑动态。作者旨在发展一个几何理论，能够在保持静态与动态套利约束的同时，统一并推广经典波动率动态模型。

Method: 将微笑动态形式化为无静态套利曲面类上的输运流：标的资产变动生成输运向量场，输运速度场v(k)作为核心对象统一各粘性制度。SSR被解释为零阶输运系数，高阶系数控制ATM偏斜、曲率及高阶微笑导数。通过局部射流输运推论将理论推广到任意对数金钱度，并在显式正则性条件下证明流局部保持蝶式与日历套利自由。实证采用序贯前向代入估计器处理五年SPX数据（七个期限，1个月至2年）。

Result: 三个主要实证发现：(1) SPX呈现超偏斜行为，SSR系数从1个月期的1.44单调降至2年期的1.01；(2) 自相似输运在所有期限被拒绝，偏斜输运系数在6个月与9个月期限之间变号；(3) 速度曲线在中期期限随金钱度显著变化，从短期的U形演变为长期单调递减。样本外测试中，完整三参数模型在中期限曲率动态上比SSR模型提升17-21%。

Conclusion: 该几何输运框架为隐含波动率动态提供了统一的理论基础，不仅涵盖了所有经典粘性制度，还通过输运系数的高阶展开提供了更丰富的描述能力。实证结果揭示了SPX波动率的超偏斜性质与期限依赖的速度曲线形态，表明传统SSR模型在描述曲率动态方面存在不足，三参数扩展模型具有显著的实证优势。

Abstract: We develop a geometric theory of arbitrage-free implied variance surface dynamics. Smile dynamics are formulated as transport flows on the admissible class of static-arbitrage-free surfaces: spot movements generate transport vector fields, and the transport velocity field v(k) unifies all classical stickiness regimes. The skew-stickiness ratio (SSR) is the zeroth-order transport coefficient; higher-order coefficients govern ATM skew, curvature, and higher smile derivatives. A local jet transport corollary extends the theory to arbitrary log-moneyness and identifies v(k) nonparametrically from market data. Under explicit regularity conditions, the flow preserves butterfly and calendar arbitrage-freeness locally. Sticky-strike, sticky-delta, SSR, local volatility, and rough volatility all arise as special cases within this framework.
  Empirically, we apply a sequential forward-substitution estimator to five years of SPX implied-volatility data across seven tenors from one month to two years. Three findings emerge. First, SPX exhibits super-skew behavior: the SSR coefficient declines monotonically from 1.44 at one month toward 1.01 at two years. Second, self-similar transport is rejected at all tenors; the skew-transport coefficient changes sign between the six- and nine-month tenors. Third, the velocity profile varies significantly with moneyness at intermediate tenors and evolves from U-shaped at short maturities to monotonically decreasing at long maturities. Out-of-sample, the full three-parameter model outperforms SSR on curvature dynamics by 17-21% at medium tenors.

</details>


### [205] [Diffusion Models in Finance: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12583)
*Zhuohan Wang,Carmine Ventre*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇专门针对扩散模型在金融数据领域应用的综述，按金融数据类型（时间序列、订单簿、表格数据及其他结构化金融对象）组织文献，并提供了开源代码库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型在金融数据建模中展现出强大的能力，如稳定的似然训练、良好的模式覆盖、灵活的条件生成以及天然契合金融领域Itô微积分和随机控制框架的SDE表述。然而，目前缺乏专门针对扩散模型在金融领域应用的系统性综述。

Method: 作者系统性地回顾了扩散族生成模型在金融应用中的现有文献，主要按金融数据类型进行分类组织，包括时间序列、限价订单簿、表格数据及其他结构化金融对象，并在每个类别中讨论了建模目标和应用场景。

Result: 完成了对扩散模型在金融领域应用的首次专门综述，构建了按数据类型分类的文献组织结构，并开源了配套的资源仓库。

Conclusion: 该综述填补了扩散模型在金融应用领域综述研究的空白，为研究人员提供了系统的文献梳理和资源汇总。

Abstract: Diffusion generative models have rapidly emerged as powerful tools for modeling complex financial data. Their appeal is both structural and practical: they offer stable likelihood-based training, strong mode coverage, flexible conditioning, and a stochastic-differential-equation formulation that aligns naturally with the Itô calculus and stochastic control frameworks widely used in finance. This survey reviews the growing literature on diffusion-family generative models for financial applications. We organize prior work primarily by financial data type, covering time series, limit order books, tabular data, and other structured financial objects, while discussing the modeling goals and application contexts that arise within each category. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first survey dedicated specifically to diffusion-family models for financial data. For more detailed information, we have open-sourced a repository https://github.com/ZhuoHan1998/Diffusion-Models-In-Finance.

</details>


### [206] [LOB-ID: Evaluating Synthetic Market Data by Inception Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13082)
*Andreea Bacalum,Zhuohan Wang,Ollie Olby,Martin Garaj,Namid Stillman*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出LOB-ID，一个基于嵌入的评估框架，将FID和MIND指标适配到限价订单簿（LOB）数据，用于评估生成式LOB模型的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式LOB模型的评估主要依赖风格化事实和选定的市场统计量，这些指标是好的诊断工具，但无法捕捉订单簿轨迹的联合时间结构与跨层级结构。

Method: 训练DeepLOB架构于五只股票四个月的Level-2订单簿数据以获得领域特定嵌入，并在此基础上适配Fréchet Inception Distance (FID)和Monge Inception Distance (MIND)指标，构建LOB-ID评估框架。

Result: LOB-ID在不同时间、工具和嵌入检查点下保持稳定，并在受控扭曲下单调上升；构造了对FID的矩匹配攻击和对基于统计评估的深度簿扰动，MIND对两种扭曲均更敏感；对五种生成式LOB模型（从随机基线到深度学习方法）的评分与各模型按构造捕捉的联合时空结构一致。

Conclusion: LOB-ID提供了比传统统计指标更全面的LOB生成模型评估方法，特别是MIND在检测微妙分布偏移方面表现更优，能为该领域提供更可靠的模型比较基准。

Abstract: Generative models of limit orderbook (LOB) data have advanced rapidly, but their evaluation often focuses on stylised facts and selected market statistics. These measures provide useful diagnostics but may not capture the joint temporal and cross-level structure of order-book trajectories. We introduce LOB-ID, an embedding-based framework that adapts the Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and Monge Inception Distance (MIND) to LOB data. To obtain domain-specific embeddings, we train the DeepLOB architecture on four months of Level-2 order-book data for five equities. We show that LOB-ID is stable across time, instruments, and embedding checkpoints, and rises monotonically under controlled distortions. We then construct a moment-matching attack against FID and a deep-book perturbation that evades statistic-based evaluation. MIND remains substantially more sensitive to both distortions. Finally, we score five generative LOB models, spanning stochastic baselines and deep learning approaches, and find that LOB-ID ranks them in line with the joint temporal and cross-level structure each captures by construction.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Weight Certificates for Convex Multi-Objective MPC: Geometric Characterization, $\ell^1$ Construction, and $\ell^2$ Foreclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12520)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Chaitanya Shinde,Miguel Hurtadoand Jerry Lopez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶规则手册中词典序规则违例排序与模型预测控制加权和近似之间的关系。作者证明，传统的分离启发式（w1≫w2≫…≫wL）回答的是错误的问题。对于凸优先级有序规划，加权和精确复现词典序最优解的条件是其权重（加上单位性能系数）支持成就映射在词典序点的上图像；可行权重构成单位性能切片的外法锥。在铰链惩罚下该切片是可通过投影缩放KKT系统获得的多面体，线性规划可返回带认证余量的内部权重；在平方铰链惩罚下，当极限乘子非零时，任何有限权重都不精确，违例沿局部极小值分支以O(1/w)衰减。在11个可校准场景类别中的9个，校准权重具有近乎相等的层级分量，在25条规则的nuPlan闭环实验中，合法层级事件精度约为匹配启发式权重的两倍。但认证是逐点的：单个权重无法在整个episode的采样tick中有效，中位寿命仅为一个采样间隔，持久性追踪活动集稳定性。这些发现支持带选择性级联回退的监控加权求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规则手册通过词典序（lexicographic）排名对规则违例进行排序，而模型预测控制（MPC）要么通过每tick运行L+1个顺序程序来精确执行该排序，要么通过分离启发式权重（w1≫w2≫…≫wL）的加权和近似执行。作者指出这种启发式方法回答的是错误的问题，需要从理论上重新审视加权和能否以及何时精确复现词典序最优解。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为凸优先级有序规划，提出"成就映射的上图像"框架来分析加权和与词典序最优解之间的关系。具体方法包括：(1) 推导可行权重的解析刻画——单位性能切片的外法锥；(2) 在铰链惩罚下，将该切片表示为投影缩放KKT系统的多面体，并通过线性规划求解内部权重及认证余量；(3) 在平方铰链惩罚下分析有限权重的非精确性及其O(1/w)衰减行为；(4) 通过在held-out日志上校准权重并设计活动集稳定性监控器，在nuPlan闭环实验中评估实际效果。

Result: 在11个可校准场景类别中的9个，校准权重具有近乎相等的层级分量（而非传统启发的w1≫w2≫…），在25条规则nuPlan闭环实验中，合法层级事件精度约为匹配启发式权重的两倍。然而，证书是逐点有效的：没有单一权重能在整个episode中有效，中位寿命仅为1个采样间隔（原生速率下为0个后续tick），持久性由活动集稳定性决定。此外，合规模式监控器只能检测到部分实测的违规。

Conclusion: 传统分离启发式权重在实践中是次优的，正确的权重可以通过几何方法（外法锥切片）求解。但精确加权在动态场景中不可持续（点态有效），因此需要监控加权求解并带有选择性级联回退的策略。监控器本身也存在覆盖不足的问题，说明需要进一步研究更全面的合规监控机制。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>


### [208] [Optimal Heat Storage Sizing for District Heating Networks to Maximize Electricity Revenue from Combined Heat and Power Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12525)
*Martin Sollich,Maarten Blommaert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数学优化的可扩展自动化方法，用于优化区域供热网络（DHN）中的短期储热系统规模。该方法对DHN、产热单元和储热设施进行非线性物理建模，以最小化总经济成本。在比利时一个含30个用户的第三代CHP-DHN案例中，最优储热整合使20年成本降低73万欧元（16.5%）。与传统简化方法相比，该整体化物理优化方法能确保设计可行性和成本评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管储热在DHN中的效益已得到公认，但由于DHN在热源、生产和消费模式、网络热损失及燃料成本等方面的多样性，确定最优储热规模仍是一个挑战。现有简化方法无法准确确定最优储热尺寸。

Method: 采用非线性物理建模方法，同时建模DHN、产热单元和储热装置，通过数学优化最小化总经济成本，显式考虑时变热生产成本、热需求和热损失，实现储热最优尺寸确定。

Result: 在比利时30用户的第三代CHP-DHN中，最优储热整合使20年成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，减少73万欧元（16.5%）。其中产热成本减少107万欧元（通过在低电价时期提取热量），储热投资为32.3万欧元。与传统简化储热尺寸方法相比，该方法能准确识别最优尺寸。

Conclusion: 所提出的整体化物理优化方法优于传统简化方法，能够确保可行设计、准确成本评估和最优储热尺寸确定，且其规模超越了以往基于物理的储热尺寸研究。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

</details>


### [209] [A Local-Linearly Convergent Algorithm for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12665)
*Frank E. Curtis,Lingjun Guo,Daniel P. Robinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的分析，证明其在强二阶稳定点附近具有局部线性收敛速率，并将其作为渐进采样策略的子问题求解器，改善了大样本等式约束优化问题的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goyens等人的Gradient-Eigenstep算法在求解非凸等式约束优化问题时具有迭代效率优势，但其收敛性分析尚不完整，且如何利用该算法处理大规模样本平均定义的优化问题也缺乏研究。
